uyartiṇiviṉi taṉmiviṉi paṭarkkiviṉi eṉa iṭattāl iruvakittu. avaṟṟuḷ taṉmiviṉi paṉmit taṉmiyum taṉittaṉmiyum eṉa iruvakippaṭum. am, ām, em, ēm, kum, ṭum, tum, ṟum eṉṉum ivveṭṭu vikutikaḷiyum iṟutiyākavuṭiya viṉiccoṟkaḷ paṉmiyuṇarttun taṉmic coṟkaḷām. taṉmik kuṟittup pēcutaṟkēṟṟa moḻivaḷam uyartiṇi māntarkkē yuriyatākaliṉ taṉmic coṟkaḷyāvum uyartiṇic collēyām. oruvaṉō oruttiyō taṉṉik kuṟittup pēcuṅkāl taṉakku orumiyallatu paṉmi collutaṟkiṭamilli. eṉiṉum taṉakku muṉṉum ayalilum uḷḷa piṟariyum taṉṉōṭu uḷappaṭuttuk kūṟum vaḻakkamuṇ miyāl taṉmip paṉmiyum koḷḷa vēṇṭiyatāyiṟṟu. ākavē paṉmit taṉmi yeṉṟatu uḷappāṭṭut taṉmiyēyām. taṉṉoṭu muṉṉiṉṟāri yuḷappaṭuttalum paṭarkkiyāri yuḷappaṭuttalum avviru tiṟattāriyum oruṅku uḷappaṭuttalum eṉa uḷappaṭuttal mūvakippaṭumeṉavum, am, ām eṉṟa vikutikaḷ muṉṉiliyāriyum tamarāyavaḻip paṭarkkiyāriyum, em, ēm eṉpaṉa paṭarkkiyāriyum, kum, ṭum, tum, ṟum eṉpaṉa avvirutiṟattāriyum uḷappaṭuttumeṉavum, am, ām, em, ēm eṉpaṉa mūṉṟu kālamum paṟṟi varutalum kum, ṭum, tum, ṟum eṉpaṉa etirkālampaṟṟi varutalum uṭiya veṉavum kūṟuvar cēṉāvariyar.
ku, ṭu, tu, ṟu eṉ, ēṉ, al eṉpavaṟṟi iṟatiyākavuṭiya ēḻum orumiyuṇarttum taṉittaṉmi (taṉmiyorumi) viṉiccoṟkaḷām. avaṟṟuḷ ceyku eṉṉum vāypāṭṭu viṉimuṟṟu viṉikoṇṭu muṭiyumāyiṉum muṟṟuc collākiya ilakkaṇattiṟ ciṟitum māṟupāṭatu.
aṉ, āṉ, aḷ, āḷ eṉṉum īṟṟiyuṭiya nālvakic coṟkaḷum uyartiṇi yorumi yuṇarttum paṭarkki viṉiccoṟkaḷām. ar, ār, pa eṉṉum īṟṟiyuṭiya mūvakic coṟkaḷum palarpāṟ paṭarkkiyām. mār eṉpatum uyartiṇip palarpāṟ paṭarkki viṉikkīṟātaliyuṭittu. atu muṭiyuṅkāl peyarkoḷḷātu viṉikoṇṭu muṭiyum. ivvāṟu am vikuti mutalāka mār vikuti yīṟākac collappaṭṭa irupattumūṉṟīṟṟu viṉiccoṟkaḷum uyartiṇikkē yuriyaṉavām.
mēṟcollappaṭṭavaṟṟuḷ paṉmiyuṇarttum taṉmic col tiṇiviravi yeṇṇuṅkāl aḵṟiṇiyi yuḷappaṭuttuttiritalum uṇṭu. yār eṉṉum viṉāviṉik kuṟippu uyartiṇi mūṉṟu pālukkum oppavuriyatākum. pāluṇarttum īṟukaḷākiya ṉa, ḷa, ra, eṉṉum iṟutiyiyuṭiya ākāramum muṉṉiliyil varum āy eṉpataṉ ākāramum ceyyuḷuḷ ōkāramāyt tiriyum.
āṟām vēṟṟumikkuriya uṭimipporuḷ, ēḻām vēṟṟumikkuriya nilapporuḷ, oppupporuḷ, paṇpu eṉṉumivaṟṟi nilikkaḷamākak koṇṭum aṉmi, iṉmi, uṇmi, vaṉmi eṉpavaṟṟi aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭum viṉikkuṟipput tōṉṟumeṉpar āciriyar kuṟippāṟ kāla muṇarttaliṉ itu kuṟippeṉappaṭṭatu. muṉṉark kūṟiya irupattumūṉṟīṟṟu viṉiccoṟkaḷukkīṟākiya eḻuttukkaḷiyē (vikutikaḷiyē) ivviṉickuṟippuc coṟkaḷum peṟṟup pāluṇarttuvaṉavām.
aḵṟiṇikkuriyaṉa
ā, ā, va eṉṉum iṟutiyiyuṭiya mūvaki viṉic coṟkaḷum aḵṟiṇippaṉmip paṭarkkiyām. tu, ṟu, ṭu eṉpavaṟṟi yiṟutiyāka uṭiyaṉa aḵṟiṇi orumippāṟkuriya viṉiccoṟkaḷām. iṅkeṭuttuk kāṭṭiya aṟuvakiyīṟṟuc coṟkaḷē aḵṟiṇikkuriya viṉiccoṟkaḷām. viṉāp poruḷiyuṭiya evaṉ eṉṉum viṉik kuṟippuccol aḵṟiṇiyirupālukkum oppavuriyatākum. iṉṟu, ila uṭiya, aṉṟu, uṭittu, alla, uḷa eṉpaṉavum paṇpukoḷ kiḷaviyum paṇpiṉākiya ciṉimutaṟ kiḷaviyum oppuppaṟṟi varuñcollum ākiya ippattum aḵṟiṇi viṉikkuṟippuc coṟkaḷām. mēl aḵṟiṇi viṉiccoṟkīṟāy niṉṟu pāluṇarttu meḻuttukkaḷē aḵṟiṇi viṉik kuṟippuc coṟkaṇṇum īṟāy niṉṟu pāl viḷakkuvaṉa.
irutiṇikkumuriyaṉa
muṉṉili viṉimuṟṟu, viyaṅkōḷ muṟṟu, viṉiyeccam, iṉmiyiyuṇarttum illi, il eṉpaṉa. vēṟu eṉṉum col, ceymmaṉ eṉṉum vāypāṭṭu muṟṟu, muṟṟum peyareccamumākiya ceyyumeṉṉum vāypāṭṭu viṉiccol, ceyta eṉṉum vāypāṭṭup peyareccam ākiya eṇvaki viṉiccoṟkaḷum irutiṇic collātaṟkum otta urimi yuṭiyaṉavām.
mēṟkūṟappaṭṭa viravu viṉikaḷuḷ i, ai, āy eṉṉum iṟutiyiyuṭiya muṉṉili viṉiccoṟkaḷ oruvaṉ, orutti, oṉṟu eṉṉum irutiṇi mukkūṟṟerumikkum oppavuriyaṉa. ir, īr, miṉ eṉṉum iṟutiyiyuṭiya muṉṉili viṉic coṟkaḷ uyartiṇip palarpālukkuma aḵṟiṇip palaviṉ pālukkum oppavuriyaṉa. muṉṉili viṉiyallāta ēṉi eḻuvaki viṉiccoṟkaḷum irutiṇi aimpāl mūviṭattiṟkum potuvāy varuvaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ viyaṅkōḷ, viṉi muṉṉili taṉmiyeṉṉum iraṇṭiṭattilum nilipeṟātu. nikaḻkāla muṇarttum ceyyumeṉṉum mūṉṟu palarpāṟ paṭarkkiyilum taṉmi muṉṉilikaḷilum varutalilli.
maṟṟoru viṉiccollōṭu kūṭiyallatu muṟṟuppeṟāta, kuṟiccol viṉiyeccamākum. ceytu, ceyyū, ceypu, ceyteṉa, ceyyiyar, ceyyiya, ceyiṉ, cey ceyaṟku eṉavarum ivvoṉpatu vāypāṭṭuc coṟkaḷum piṉ, muṉ, kāl, kaṭi, vaḻi, iṭattu eṉpavaṟṟi yiṟutiyāka vuṭiyaṉavum ivipōlak kālamuṇartti varuvaṉa piṟavum viṉiyecca viṉikaḷām. ivaṟṟuḷ mutaliluḷḷa ceytu, ceyyū, ceypu eṉa varum mūṉṟum taṉakkuriya viṉi mutal viṉiyiyē koṇṭu muṭivaṉa. avi mūṉṟum ciṉi viṉiyāṅkāl tamakkuriya mutal viṉiyikkoṇṭu muṭiyuṉum tamtoḻilik koṇṭu muṭintaṉavē. immūṉṟumallāta piṟa viṉiyeccaṅkaḷ tam viṉi mutal viṉiyiyēṉum aṉṟi aṅku vantu iyiyum piṟa viṉimutal viṉiyiyeṉum variyaṟiyiṉṟik koṇṭu muṭiyumiyalpiṉavām. viṉiyeccaṅkaḷ pala vāypāṭṭāṉ aṭukkivariṉum muṉṉiṉṟa eccam muṭiya ēṉiyavum poruḷāl muṭintaṉavēyām.
peyari oḻipākavuṭiya viṉiccol peyareccam atu ceyyum, ceyta eṉavarum iru vāypāṭukaḷil aṭaṅkum nilappeyar, poruṭpeyar, kālappeyar, karuvippeyar, viṉi mutaṟpeyar, viṉippeyar eṉavarum aṟuvakip peyarkaḷiyum koṇṭu muṭitaṟkēṟṟa poruḷ nilimiyiyuṭiya ippeyareccam irutiṇi yimpāṟku muriya potu viṉiyākum. ceyyum eṉṉum vāypāṭṭuc coṟkaḷ peyareccamāṅkāl muṉ ceyyumeṉṉum muṟṟiṟku vilakkappaṭṭa pallōr paṭarkki muṉṉili, taṉmi eṉṉum ivviṭaṅkaḷukkum uriyaṉavām.
peyareccamum viṉiyeccamum viṉi nikaḻcciyi yuṇarttātu ataṉi etirmaṟuttuc colliṉum peyareñca niṟṟalum viṉiyeñca niṟṟalumākiya tam poruḷ nilimiyil vēṟupaṭā. ivveccaṅkaḷukkum ivaṟṟi muṭikkuñ coṟkaḷāy varum peyar viṉikaḷukkumiṭiyē muṭikkuñcolloṭu toṭarpuṭiya vēṟu col vantu niṟṟalumuṇṭu.
ceyyumeṉṉum peyarecca vāypāṭukaḷiṉ īṟṟayaleḻut tākiya ukaram tāṉ ūrntuniṉṟa meyyoṭuṅ keṭṭu muṭitalum uṇṭu. ceytu eṉṉum vāyppāṭṭu iṟantakāla viṉiyeccam ēṉik kālac collōṭu iyitalum, viriviṉkaṇ etirkālamum nikaḻkālamum iṟantakālattōṭu mayaṅkutalum, mikkatu oṉṟiṉkaṇṇē iṟantakālamum etirkālamum nikaḻkālattōṭu mayaṅkutalum, itu ceyal vēṇṭum eṉṉum poruḷil varum viṉiccol tāṉum piṟarumākiya īriṭattum poruḷ tarutalum, vaṟpuṟuttalil varum viṉāp poruḷiyuṭiya viṉiccol etirmaṟip poruḷ tarutalum, iyaṟki paṟṟiyum teḷivupaṟṟiyum kālam mayaṅkutalum, ceyappaṭuporuḷ viṉimutalpōla varutalum, ivvāṟē vēṟiṭaṅkaḷil mukkālamu mayaṅkutalum ākiya vitikaḷi ivviyal 40-mutal 49-variyuḷḷa cūttiraṅkaḷāl āciriyar virittuk kūṟiyuḷḷār.
- ka. veḷḷivāraṇaṉār 
nūlvarici 10, pak. 208-213
āṟāvatu
viṉiyiyal
200.	viṉiyeṉap paṭuvatu vēṟṟumi koḷḷātu
	niṉiyuṅ kālik kālamoṭu tōṉṟum.
eṉpatu cūttiram. itu, niṟuttamuṟiyāṉē viṉi yilakkaṇam uṇarttiṉamiyiṉ, ivvōttu viṉiyiyaleṉak kāraṇappeyar peṟṟatu. iccūttiram, viṉiccoṟ kellām potuilakkaṇam uṇarttukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) viṉiyeṉappaṭuvatu vēṟṟumi koḷḷātu - viṉiccolleṉṟu collappaṭuvatu vēṟṟumiyōṭu poruntātu, niṉiyuṅ kālik kālamoṭu tōṉṟum - ārāyuṅkāṟ kālattōṭu pulappaṭum, e-ṟu.
‘viṉi’ eṉṟatu, mutaṉiliyi. iḵtu ākupeyarāyt taṉṉāṟ piṟakkiṉṟa colliyuṇarttiṟṟu.
uṇ, tiṉ, karu, cey eṉpaṉa viṉi. uṇṭal, tiṉṟal, karumi, cemmi eṉpaṉa am mutalniliyāṟ piṟanta viṉippeyar. uṇṭāṉ, tiṉṟāṉ, kariyaṉ, ceyyaṉ eṉpaṉa ammutaṉiliyāṟ piṟanta viṉiccol. iṅṅaṉam viṉiyāṟ piṟattaliṉ, ‘viṉiyiyaleṉṟār.	(1)
201.	kālan tāmē mūṉṟeṉa moḻipa.
itu, muṟkūṟiya kālam iṉitteṉkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) kālantāmē mūṉṟu eṉa moḻipa - mēṟ tōṟṟuvāy ceyyappaṭṭa kālantām mūṉṟu eṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar, e-ṟu.	(2)
202.	iṟappi ṉikaḻvi ṉetirvi ṉeṉṟā
	vammuk kālamuṅ kuṟippoṭuṅ koḷḷu
	meynnili yuṭiya tōṉṟa lāṟē.
iḵtu, akkālattiṟkup peyarum muṟiyun tokiyuṅ kūṟi, atu tāṉ kuṟippiṟkum uṇṭeṉkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) iṟappiṉ nikaḻviṉ etirviṉ eṉṟā ammuk kālamum - iṟappum nikaḻvum etirvumeṉṟu collappaṭum ammūṉṟu kālamum, kuṟippoṭuṅ koḷḷum meynnili uṭiya - viṉikkuṟippōṭum poruntum uṇmi nilimiyiyuṭiya, tōṉṟalāṟē - viṉic collāṉ akkālaṅkaḷ tōṉṟum neṟikkaṇ, e-ṟu.
(u-m.) uṇṭāṉ, uṇṇāniṉṟāṉ, uṇpāṉ, eṉak kālam mūṉṟun tōṉṟiniṉṟavāṟu kāṇka. kariyaṉ, ceyyaṉ eṉpaṉa viṉikkuṟippu.
iṟappāvatu, toḻilatu kaḻivu; nikaḻvāvatu, toḻil toṭaṅki muṟṟuppeṟāmal naṭakkiṉṟa nilimi; etirvāvatu, toḻil piṟavāmi.
toḻilāvatu, poruḷiṉatu puṭipeyarcci. iḵtu iraṇṭu kaṇaṅkālattiṉkaṇ nillāmiyiṉ, nikaḻcci yeṉpatu oṉṟu iṉṟāyiṉum, ‘uṇṇāniṉṟāṉeṉap paḵṟoḻiṟṟokutipaṟṟi nikaḻcciyum uṭittāyiṟṟu. itaṉāṉē, ‘nikaḻ kālam iṉṟeṉpārum, ‘nikaḻkālam oṉṟumē uṇṭeṉpārumeṉap pala pakutiyar āciriyareṉpatu koḷka.	(3)
203.	kuṟippiṉum viṉiyiṉu neṟippaṭat tōṉṟik
	kālamoṭu varūum viṉiccol lellā
	muyartiṇik kurimiyu maḵṟiṇik kurimiyu
	māyiru tiṇikkumō raṉṉa vurimiyu
	mammū vurupiṉa tōṉṟa lāṟē.
itu, viṉiccoṟkaḷatu pākupāṭu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) kuṟippiṉum viṉiyiṉum neṟippaṭat tōṉṟik kālamoṭu varūum viṉiccolellām - kuṟippup poruṇmikkaṇṇun toḻiṟporuṇmikkaṇṇum muṟimippaṭat tōṉṟik kālattōṭu varum ellā viṉiccollum, uyartiṇikku urimiyum aḵṟiṇikku urimiyum āyirutiṇikkumōraṉṉa urimiyum - uyar tiṇikku urimiyuṭimiyum aḵṟiṇikku urimi yuṭimiyum avviraṇṭu tiṇikkum oppa urimi yuṭimiyumeṉa, ammūuruviṉa tōṉṟalāṟē - ammūṉṟu kūṟṟiṉiyuṭiyavān tōṉṟu neṟikkaṇ, e-ṟu.
(u-m.) uṇṭāṉ, kariyaṉ; ceṉṟatu, ceyyatu; vantaṉi, veḷiyi eṉa varum. ‘kālamoṭu varumeṉap piṉṉuṅ kūṟiṉār, kuṟippiṟkuṅ kālam uṇṭu eṉpatu valiyuṟuttaṟku.
immukkālattum varutaṟku uriya kālaṅ kāṭṭum eḻuttukkaḷi āciriyar eṭuttōtārāyiṉār, piṉṉuḷḷōr kālaṅkaṭku uriyavāka eṭuttōtiya eḻuttukkaḷ tammuḷ mayaṅkiyum varutaliṉ. āciriyar, aṅṅaṉam mayaṅkutalpaṟṟi eṭuttōtāmiyiṉ, uriyāciriyarum avveḻuttukkaḷic ciṟitē kāṭṭiṉār.
iṉip piṉṉuḷḷōr kūṟiṉāreṉpatupaṟṟiyum, māṇākkarkku uyttuṇarum uṇarvu iṉṟu eṉpatupaṟṟiyum, yāmum avveḻuttukkaḷip pirittuk kāṭṭutum. cēṉāvariyar avvāṟu pirittuk kāṭṭukiṉṟavar, ‘neṟippaṭattōṉṟi’ eṉpataṉāṟ kāṭṭuvar. atu poruntātu, āciriyar eṭuttōtāmal ‘mayaṅkume eṉṟu pōtaliṉ, itaṉic ‘ceyyuṇ maruṅkiṉum’ eṉṉum atikārap puṟaṉaṭiyiṟ ‘pirittaṉar kāṭṭal’ eṉpataṉāṟ pirittuk kāṭṭutum. atu mēṟkaṇṭu koḷka.	(4)
204.	avitām,
	ammā memē meṉṉuṅ kiḷaviyu
	mummoṭu varūuṅ kaṭataṟa veṉṉu
	mannāṟ kiḷaviyo ṭāyeṇ kiḷaviyum
	paṉmi yurikkun taṉmic collē.
itu, muṟiyāṉē uyartiṇi muṟṟuviṉi kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) avitām - mēl pakukkappaṭṭa viṉiyiṉ muppāṟ pākupāṭumākiya avitām ākumāṟu iṉiccolluval am ām em ēm eṉṉuṅ kiḷaviyum - am, ām, em, ēm eṉṉum īṟṟavākiya collum, ummoṭu varūum ka ṭa ta ṟa eṉṉum annāṟ kiḷaviyoṭu - um eṉṉum iṟutiyiṭiccolliṉ ukaraṅ ka, ṭa, ta, ṟa eṉṉum oṟṟiṉmēl ēṟiya kummum ṭummum tummun ṟummum eṉṉum īṟṟavākiya annāṉku collum, āyeṇ kiḷaviyum - ākiya avveṭṭuc collum paṉmi urikkun taṉmic collē - colluvāṉ taṉṉoṭu piṟariyuṅ kūṭṭip paṉmiyiṉic collun taṉmikku uriya collām, e-ṟu.
iccūttiram mutalāka, ‘muṉṉili viyaṅkōḷ’ īṟāka, muṟṟu uṇarttukiṉṟār, viṉiyeccamum peyareccamum oḻittu. iccūttirattāṟ paṉmittaṉmiyum mēlic cūttirattāṉ orumit taṉmiyum uṇarttukiṉṟār.
muṉṉar niṉṟa nāṉkum iṟantakālampaṟṟi varuṅkāl ‘am, ām’ eṉpaṉa taṉṉōṭu muṉṉiṉṟāriyun tamarāyavaḻip paṭarkkiyāriyum uḷappaṭutti, kaṭataṟa eṉṉuṅ kāla eḻuttiṉmuṉ am ‘aṉ’ peṟṟum, ām ‘aṉ’ peṟātum varum. ‘em, ēm’ eṉpaṉa, paṭarkkiyāri uḷappaṭutti, akkāla eḻuttiṉmuṉṉar em ‘aṉ’ peṟṟum ēm ‘aṉ’ peṟṟum peṟātum varum.
(u-m.) nakkaṉam, uṇṭaṉam, urittaṉam, tiṉṟaṉam; nakkilam, uṇṭilam, urittilam, tiṉṟilam; nakkaṉamallam, uṇṭaṉamallam, urittaṉamallam, tiṉṟaṉamallam eṉavum, nakkām, uṇṭām, urit tām, tiṉṟām; nakkilām, uṇṭilām, urittilām, tiṉṟilām; nakkāmallām, uṇṭāmallām, urittāmallām, tiṉṟāmallām eṉavum nakkaṉem, uṇṭaṉem, urittaṉem, tiṉṟaṉem; nakkilem, uṇṭilem, urittilem, tiṉṟilem : nakkaṉemallem, uṇṭaṉe mallem, urittaṉemallem, tiṉṟaṉemallem eṉavum, nakkaṉēm, nakkēm; uṇṭaṉēm, uṇṭēm, urittaṉēm, urittēm; tiṉṟaṉēm, tiṉṟēm; nakkilēm, uṇṭilēm, urittilēm, tiṉṟilēm; nakkēmallēm, uṇṭēmallēm, urittēmallēm, tiṉṟēmallēm eṉavum varum.
iṉi, ‘am, ām’ eṉpaṉavaṟṟiṟku, nīyum yāṉu muṇṭaṉam; nīyum yāṉum uṇṭām; nīyum yāṉumavaṉum uṇṭaṉam; nīyumyāṉum avaṉumuṇṭām eṉa varum. ēṉiyavaṟṟōṭum oṭṭuka. ‘em, ēm’ eṉpaṉavaṟṟiṟku,  yāṉum avaṉumuṇṭaṉem; yāṉumuvaṉumuṇṭaṉēm, yāṉumuvaṉum uṇṭēm eṉa varum. ēṉiyavaṟṟōṭum oṭṭuka.
annāṉku īṟum ēṉiyeḻuttiṉmuṉ ṅakāramum ḻakāramum oḻittu ‘iṉ’ peṟṟu varum. añciṉam, tappiṉam eṉavum; uriñiṉam, eṇṇiṉam, poruniṉam, tirumiṉam, paṉṉiṉam eṉavum, pōyiṉam; vāriṉam, colliṉam, mēviṉam, eḷḷiṉam eṉavum varum. oḻinta mūvīṟṟōṭum oṭṭuka. kalak kiṉam, teruṭṭiṉam, aruttiṉam, araṟṟiṉam eṉpaṉa, kakara īṟum ṭakara īṟum takara īṟum ṟakara īṟum mutaṉilic collāy niṉṟu ‘iṉ’ peṟṟaṉa.
iṉi, ivi nikaḻkālam paṟṟi varuṅkāl ‘nil, kiṉṟu’ eṉpavaṟṟōṭu vantu, muṟkūṟiyavāṟē niṟkum. ‘nilleṉpatu lakaram ṉakaramāyt taṉakkēṟṟa ṟakaram peṟṟu niṟkum. iṉi, ‘niṉ, kiṉ’ eṉa niṟkumeṉṟumām.  uṇṇāniṉṟaṉam, uṇkiṉṟaṉam; uṇṇāniṉṟilam, uṇ kiṉṟilam eṉavum, uṇṇāniṉṟām, uṇkiṉṟām; uṇṇāniṉṟilām, uṇkiṉṟilām eṉavum, uṇṇāniṉṟaṉem, uṇkiṉṟaṉem; uṇṇā niṉṟilem, uṇkiṉṟilem eṉavum, uṇṇāniṉṟaṉēm, uṇkiṉṟaṉēm uṇṇāniṉṟēm, uṇkiṉṟēm; uṇṇāniṉṟilēm, uṇkiṉṟilēm eṉavum varum.
uṇṇākiṭantāṉ, uṇṇāviruntāṉ eṉak ‘kiṭa, iru’ veṉpaṉavum nikaḻkālattu varum.
uṇkiṟēṉeṉak ‘kiṟu’ eṉpatu nikaḻkālam uṇarttutal ikkāla vaḻakku.
iṉi, ivi etirkālam paṟṟi varuṅkāṟ pakaramum vakaramum peṟṟu varum. vakaram ēṟpuḻik kukaramum ukaramum aṭuttum niṟkum. uṇpam, uṇkuvam, uriñuvam, tirumuvam, uṇṇalam eṉavum; ‘uṇpām, uṇkuvām, uṇṇām eṉavum; uṇpēm uṇkuvēm, uṇṇalēm eṉavum; uṇpēm, uṇṇēm eṉavum varum.
iṉi, ‘am’ īṟu varuṅkāl, antaṇāḷaṉi nakukam yāmē.’ eṉavum, ‘pāṭukam vammiṉ pōtukam.’  eṉavum, ‘pāṭukam vāvāḻi tōḻi,’ eṉavum kakaram etirkālamum uṇartti varum. kūṟuvaṉ, colluvaṉ, pōtuvaṉ eṉa varum vakaramum etirkālam uṇarttum.
‘iṉi, ‘ummoṭu varūuṅ kaṭataṟa,’ uṇkum, uṇṭum, varutum, cēṟum eṉa varum. yāṉum nīyumuṇkum; yāṉu mavaṉuṉiyumuṇkum eṉa ellāvaṟṟōṭumoṭṭuka.
ik ka ṭa ta ṟa kkaḷ mutaṉiliyi aṭuttu varuṅkāl iṟappum, īṟṟiṉi aṭuttu varuṅkāl etirvum uṇarttum. iṉi, ivaṟṟuṭ ciṟupāṉmi nikaḻkālam uṇarttuvaṉavum uḷa. atu, ‘ciṟukaṇ yāṉiyoṭu peruntēreyti, yāmava ṇiṉṟum varutum’ eṉa varum. iṉi, ‘uriñutum, tirumutum’ eṉa ēṉi eḻuttiṉmuṉ ukaram peṟṟu varum.	(5)
205.	kaṭataṟa veṉṉu
	mannāṉ kūrnta kuṉṟiya lukaramō
	ṭeṉṉē ṉalleṉa varūu mēḻun
	taṉviṉi yurikkun taṉmic collē.
itu, taṉittaṉmiyākiya muṟṟuk kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) ka ṭa ta ṟa eṉṉum annāṉku ūrnta kuṉṟiya lukaramoṭu - ka, ṭa, ta, ṟa eṉṉum annāṉku meyyiyum ūrntu vanta kuṟṟiyalukarat tiṉi īṟākavuṭiya annāṉku collum, eṉ ēṉ, al eṉa varūum ēḻum - eṉ, ēṉ, al eṉṟu colla varukiṉṟa mūṉṟu īṟṟuccollu mākiya avvēḻun taṉ viṉi urikkun taṉmiccollē - piṟari uḷappaṭuttātu taṉ viṉiyi uṇarttun taṉmiccollām, e-ṟu.
kuṟṟukaram nāṉkum, perumpāṉmiyum etirkālattuñ ciṟupāṉmi ēṉikkālattum varum.
(u-m.) uṇku, uṇṭu, varutu, cēṟu, uriñuku, tirumuku eṉa ‘ummīṟu pōla etirkālaṅ kāṭṭiṉa. ‘kaḻintatu poḻinteṉa vāṉkaṇ māṟiṉum, tollatu viḷinteṉa nilamvaḷaṅ kāppiṉum’ eṉpuḻip ‘poḻinteṉavum, ‘viḷinteṉavum iṟantakālam uṇarttiṉa. nikaḻkālam vantuḻik kāṇka. ‘aḻāaṟkō viṉiyē nōy nontuṟivi’ eṉṉuṅ kuṟuntokip pāṭṭiṉuḷ aḻuteṉṉum uṭampāṭṭiṟku aḻāaṟkeṉa etirmaṟi vantatu.
iṉi, ōci vēṟṟumiyāṟ, kakara ukaramum ṟakara ukaramum viyaṅkōṇ muṟṟāyum niṟkum. ‘neṭuntēr tāṅkumati valava! veṉṟi ḻintaṉaṉ’ eṉavum ‘tāṅkuniṉṉivalameṉṟīr’ eṉavum ‘nīkūṟum poyccūḷaṇaṅkāyiṉ maṟṟitu, yārmēl viḷiyumō? kūṟu’ eṉavum varum. ‘eṉ, ēṉ’ eṉpaṉa, mūṉṟukālamum paṟṟi varum. ivi kāla eḻuttup peṟuṅkāl ‘eṉ’ īṟu ‘em’ īṟṟōṭum, ‘ēṉ’ īṟu ‘ēm’ īṟṟōṭum okkum. uṇṭaṉeṉ, uṇṭileṉ, uṇṭaṉeṉalleṉ; uṇṇāniṉṟaṉeṉ, uṇkiṉṟaṉeṉ, uṇṇāniṉṟileṉ, uṇkiṉṟileṉ; uṇpeṉ, uṇkuveṉ, uṇṇaleṉ eṉavum, uṇṭaṉēṉ, uṇṭēṉ, uṇṭilēṉ, uṇṭaṉēṉallēṉ; uṇṇā niṉṟaṉēṉ, uṇṇāniṉṟēṉ, uṇkiṉṟaṉēṉ, uṇkiṉṟēṉ, uṇṇāniṉṟilēṉ, uṇkiṉṟilēṉ; uṇpēṉ, uṇkuvēṉ, uṇṇēṉ eṉavum varum.
iṉip pakara vakaraṅkaḷ nikaḻkālattum varum. ‘kūṭanīr niṉṟa peṟṟi kaṇṭippāṉōkkuvēṟku’ eṉpuḻip ‘pārkkiṉṟēṟku’ eṉap poruḷ tantavāṟu kāṇka. pakaram vantuḻik kāṇka.
‘al’ īṟu, pakara vakaram peṟṟu, etirkālattu varum. uṇpal, varuval eṉa varum; uṇṇāniṟpaleṉac ciṟupāṉmi nikaḻkālamum peṟum. oḻival, tavirval eṉa varum maṟi vāypāṭum aṟika.
viṉikoṇṭu muṭiyuñ ceyku eṉpataṉi um īṟṟōṭu iyiya vittamiyāṟ ceykumeṉpatūuṅ kāṇkum vantēmeṉa viṉi koṇṭu muṭiyum.	(6)
206.	avaṟṟuṭ,
	ceykeṉ kiḷavi viṉiyoṭu muṭiyiṉu
	mavviya ṟiriyā teṉmaṉār pulavar.
iḵtu, avaṟṟuṭ kakara ukara īṟṟiṟku muṭipu vēṟṟumi kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) avaṟṟuḷ - muṟkūṟiya orumittaṉmi viṉi ēḻaṉuḷ, ceykeṉ kiḷavi viṉiyoṭu muṭiyiṉum - ceykeṉṉum muṟṟuc coṟ peyarōṭu muṭiyātu viṉiyōṭu muṭiyiṉum, avviyal tiriyātu eṉmaṉār pulavar - ammuṟṟuc collān taṉmiyiṟ tiriyātu eṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar, e-ṟu.
(u-m.) ‘kāṇku vanticiṉ peruma! eṉa varum. ‘nōkō yāṉē’ eṉap peyar kōṭal ciṟupāṉmiyām.	(7)
207.	aṉṉā ṉaḷḷā ḷeṉṉu nāṉku
	moruvar maruṅkiṟ paṭarkkic collē.
itu, paṭarkki yorumi muṟṟu uṇarttukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) aṉ āṉ aḷ āḷ eṉṉum nāṉkum - aṉ, āṉ, aḷ, āḷ eṉṉum īṟṟiyuṭiya nāṉku collum, oruvar maruṅkiṟ paṭarkkic collē - oruvaṉ, oruttiyeṉṉum uyartiṇi yorumip pāliṭattup paṭarkkiyi uṇarttuñ collām, e-ṟu.
innāṉku īṟum mūṉṟukālamum paṟṟi varuṅkāl aṉṉum aḷḷum amīṟṟōṭum, āṉum āḷum āmīṟṟōṭum okkum.
(u-m.) uṇṭaṉaṉ, uṇṭilaṉ; uṇṇāniṉṟaṉaṉ, uṇkiṉṟaṉaṉ, uṇṇāniṉṟilaṉ, uṇkiṉṟilaṉ; uṇpaṉ, uṇkuvaṉ, uṇṇalaṉ eṉavum, uṇṭāṉ, uṇṭilāṉ; uṇṇāniṉṟāṉ, uṇkiṉṟāṉ, uṇṇāniṉṟilāṉ, uṇkiṉṟilāṉ; uṇpāṉ, uṇkuvāṉ, uṇ ṇāṉ eṉavum, uṇṭaṉaḷ, uṇṭilaḷ; uṇṇāniṉṟaṉaḷ, uṇkiṉṟaṉaḷ, uṇṇāniṉṟilaḷ, uṇkiṉṟilaḷ; uṇpaḷ, uṇkuvaḷ, uṇṇalaḷ eṉavum, uṇṭāḷ, uṇṭilāḷ; uṇṇā niṉṟāḷ, uṇkiṉṟāḷ, uṇṇāniṉṟilāḷ, uṇkiṉṟilāḷ; uṇpāḷ, uṇkuvāḷ, uṇṇāḷ eṉavum varum, uṇṭaṉa ṉallaṉ, uṇṭāṉallaṉ; uṇṭaṉaḷallaḷ, uṇṭāṉallaḷ eṉap piṟa vāypāṭṭāṉvarum maṟiyum aṟika.	(8)
208.	arār pa eṉa varūum mūṉṟum
	pallōr maruṅkiṟ paṭarkkic collē.
itu, paṭarkkippaṉmi muṟṟu uṇarttukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) arār pa eṉa varūum mūṉṟum - ar, ār, pa eṉṉum īṟṟeḻuttiṉiyuṭiyavāy varum mūṉṟu collum, pallōr maruṅkiṟ paṭarkkic collē - pallōriṭattup paṭarkkip paṉmiyi uṇarttuñ collām, e-ṟu.
ar, ār eṉṉum iraṇṭum, mūṉṟu kālamum paṟṟi varum. ivi kāleḻuttup peṟuṅkāl aṉ īṟṟōṭu ar īṟum, āṉ īṟṟōṭu ār īṟum okkum.
(u-m.) uṇṭaṉar, uṇṭilar; uṇṇāniṉṟaṉar, uṇkiṉṟaṉar, uṇṇāniṉṟilar, uṇkiṉṟilar; uṇpar, uṇkuvar uṇṇalar eṉavum, uṇṭār, uṇṭilār; uṇṇā niṉṟār, uṇkiṉṟār, uṇṇāniṉṟilār, uṇkiṉṟilār; uṇpār, uṇkuvār, uṇṇār eṉavum varum. uṇṭaṉarallar, uṇṭārallar eṉa varum maṟi vikaṟpamum aṟika.
pakaram etirkālam paṟṟi varuṅkāl ukaramuṅkukaramum aṭuttum aṭātum niṟkum. uriñupa varukupa eṉavum, uṇpa eṉavum varum. oḻipa, tavirpa eṉa maṟiyum varum. uṇṇā toḻipa eṉpatu ellāvaṟṟiṟkum potu. iḵtu, uṇṇāniṟpa eṉa nikaḻkālattum varum.	(9)
209.	mārik kiḷaviyum pallōr paṭarkki
	kālak kiḷaviyoṭu muṭiyu meṉpa.
ituvum, paṉmikku uriya īṟum ataṉ muṭipu vēṟṟumiyuṅ kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) mārik kiḷaviyum pallōr paṭarkki - muṉṉiyaṉavēyaṉṟi, mār īṟṟuc collum pallōri uṇarttum paṭarkkiccollām; kālak kiḷaviyoṭu muṭiyum eṉpa - aḵtu avipōlap peyar koḷḷātu viṉikoṇṭu muṭiyum, e-ṟu.
‘mār’ ukaram aṭuttum aṭātum niṟkum.
(u-m.) ‘eḷḷumār vantārēyīṅku,’ (kali. 81 : 24) ‘ārttār koṇmār vantār’ eṉa varum. iḵtu etirkālam. ‘nilavaṉ mārē pulavar’ ‘pāṭiṉ maṉṉarip pāṭaṉmāremarē’‘nōymali varuttaṅ kāṇaṉmāremarē’eṉpaṉa, ‘nilavuka, pāṭuka, kāṇka,’ eṉṉum viyaṅkōṭku etirmaṟi yeṉṟu uṇarka. iṉi,  ‘nilavuvār, pāṭuvār, kāṇpār’ eṉpaṉa cila viyaṅkōṇ muṟṟeṉṟu kūṟi, ataṟku etirmaṟiyāy, nilavātoḻivār, pāṭātoḻivār, kāṇātoḻivār eṉa ēvaṟ poruṇmiyi uṇartti niṉṟaṉa eṉṟalum oṉṟu.	 (10)
210.	paṉmiyu morumiyum pālaṟi vanta
	vannā lintu mūṉṟutali yiṭṭa
	muṉṉuṟak kiḷanta vuyartiṇi yavvē.
itu, ‘virintatu tokuttut’ terinili viṉikaḷ uyartiṇikku uriya eṉkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) paṉmiyum orumiyum pāl aṟivanta mūṉṟu taliyiṭṭa annālintum - paṉmiyum orumiyumākiya pāl aṟiya vanta irupattu mūṉṟu īṟṟu viṉiccollum, muṉṉuṟakkiḷanta uyartiṇiya - kiḷaviyākkattuk kūṟappaṭṭa uyartiṇiyi uṭiyaṉavām, e-ṟu.
īṇṭuk kūṟiyataṉāṟ payaṉ, aṉ, āṉ mutaliya iṭiccoṟpaṟṟi uyartiṇippaṭarkki viṉimuṟṟu uṇarttutal; āṇṭuk kūṟiyataṉāṟ payaṉ, vaḻukkāttaṟku avaṟṟiṉ īṟukaḷit tokuttu ilakkaṇa vaḻakku uṇarttutal eṉṟuṇarka.	(11)
211.	avaṟṟuḷ,
	paṉmi yurikkun taṉmik kiḷavi
	yeṇṇiyaṉ maruṅkiṟ ṟiripavi yuḷavē.
iḵtu, uḷappāṭṭut taṉmikkut tiripu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) avaṟṟuḷ paṉmi urikkun taṉmik kiḷavi - muṟkūṟappaṭṭa irupattumūṉṟu coṟkaḷuṭ paṉmiyi uṇarttun taṉmic col, eṇ iyal maruṅkiṟ 
tiripavi uḷa - eṇṇutal naṭakkum iṭattu aḵṟiṇiyi uḷappaṭuttit tirivaṉa uḷa, e-ṟu.
(u-m.) ‘yāṉumeṉ ṉeḵkamuñ cāṟum. iḵtu aḵṟiṇiyi uḷappaṭuttal ‘taṉmiccol’ eṉṉuñ cūttirattāṉ vantatu.	(12)
212.	yāa reṉṉum viṉāviṉ kiḷavi
	yattiṇi maruṅkiṉ muppāṟku murittē.
iḵtu, uyartiṇi viṉikkuṟippiṉuḷ oṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) yāar eṉṉum viṉāviṉ kiḷavi attiṇi maruṅkiṉ muppāṟkum urittē - yāreṉṉum viṉāpporuḷi uṇarttuñ col uyartiṇi maruṅkiṉ mūṉṟu pāṟkum urittu, e-ṟu.
(u-m.) avaṉ yār? avaḷ yār? avar yār?
itu, viṉik kuṟippāyiṉum, pallōr paṭarkkiyi uṇarttum ār īṟṟāṉ mūṉṟu pāliyum uṇarttutaliṉ, avaṟṟoṭu viyātu īṇṭu vittār. ‘uyar tiṇikku uritteṉavē, aḵṟiṇikku urittaṉṟic ciṟupāṉmi varutal koḷka. aḵtu, ‘ūtikūṭṭuṇṇumukupaṉi yāmateṅ - kōtikūṭ ṭuṇṇiya tāṉyārmaṉ - pōtelān - tātoṭu tāḻuntārk kacci vaḷanāṭaṉ - ṟūtoṭu vārāta vaṇṭu?’ eṉa uṇṇiya vaṇṭutāṉ yāreṉa aḵṟiṇikkaṇ vantatu. itu tiṇi vaḻuvamiti eṉṟalum oṉṟu.	(13)
213.	pālaṟi marapi ṉammū vīṟṟu
	māvō vākuñ ceyyu ḷuḷḷē.
iḵtu, avvirupattumūṉṟu īṟṟiṉuḷ mūṉṟīṟu ceyyuḷuṭ tiriyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) pāl aṟi marapiṉ ammūvīṟṟum - pāl viḷaṅka varum iyalpiyuṭiya āṉ, āḷ, ār eṉṉum mūṉṟu īṟṟiṉkaṇṇum, ā ō ākuñ ceyyuḷuḷḷē - ākāram ōkāramāñ ceyyuḷiṭattu, e-ṟu.
(u-m.) ‘viṉavi niṟṟantōṉē’ nalli maṉṉeṉa nakūuppeyarn tōḷē’ ‘vāṭā vaḷḷiyaṅ kāṭiṟan tōrē’  eṉa varum. vantōm, ceṉṟōm eṉṉum vaḻakku ēm īṟṟiṉ citiveṉa maṟukka. 	(14)
214.	āyeṉ kiḷaviyu mavaṟṟoṭu koḷḷum.
itu, viravuviṉiyum ammuṭipu peṟum eṉkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) āy eṉ kiḷaviyum avaṟṟoṭu koḷḷum - muṉṉili yīṟṟuḷ āyeṉṉum īṟum mēṟkūṟappaṭṭaṉapōla ākāram ōkāramāñ ceyyuḷuḷ, e-ṟu.
(u-m.) vantōy maṉṟa taṇkaṭaṟ cērppa eṉa varum.
ivviravuviṉi uyartiṇimēl vantāl ā ō vāme eṉṟaṟkum, ā ō ātal atikārappaṭutalāṉum īṇṭuk kūṟiṉār. ivi toḻiṟpeyarāyiṉ, peyariyaluḷ aṭaṅkum.	(15)
215.	atuccol vēṟṟumi yuṭimi yāṉum
	kaṇṇeṉ vēṟṟumi nilatti ṉāṉum
	moppi ṉāṉum paṇpi ṉāṉumeṉ
	ṟappāṟ kālaṅ kuṟippoṭu tōṉṟum.
itu, niṟuttamuṟiyāṉē uyartiṇi viṉikkuṟippu uṇarttukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) atuccol vēṟṟumi uṭimiyāṉum - āṟām vēṟṟumiyatu uṭimipporuṭkaṇṇum, kaṇ eṉ vēṟṟumi nilattiṉāṉum - ēḻām vēṟṟumiyatu nilap poruṇmikkaṇṇum, oppiṉāṉum - uvamattiṉkaṇṇum, paṇpiṉāṉum - paṇpiṉkaṇṇum, eṉṟu appāṟkālaṅ kuṟippoṭu tōṉṟum - eṉa apporuṭpakuti paṟṟi varuñ collakattuk kālaṅ kuṟippāṟ tōṉṟum, e-ṟu.
‘uṭimiyeṉpatu, uṭimittaṉmiyum uṭimip poruḷumeṉa iruvakippaṭum. uṭimittaṉmiyāvatu, taṉ celvatti niṉintu iṉpuṟutaṟku ētuvākiya paṟṟuḷḷam. aḵtu, ‘āṅkaviyorupālāka’ eṉṉum meyppāṭṭiyaṟ cūttirattu ‘uṭimi yiṉpuṟal’ eṉpataṉāṉ uṇarka. uṭimip poruḷāvatu, oṉṟaṟku oṉṟi urimi ceytu niṟpatu. aḵtu, ipporuḷiṉuṭiyatu ip poru ḷeṉṟum, ‘ipporuḷ ipporuḷiṉuṭimi yāyirunta teṉṟum, ipporuḷiyuṭiyatāyiruntatu ipporuḷ eṉṟum mūṉṟu vakippaṭum. avi muṟiyē ‘cāttaṉatu āṭi’ yeṉa āṟaṉurupāyum, ‘āṭi cāttaṉateṉavuṅkuḻiyaṉ, kacciṉa ṉeṉavum viṉikkuṟippāyum niṟkum. urupu uṇarttumvaḻi āṭi mutaliyaṉa piṉ ṉiṟkumāṟum, kuṟippu uṇarttum vaḻi āṭi mutaliyaṉa muṉ ṉiṟkumāṟum uṇarka. ‘kuḻiyaṉeṉpuḻi, mēlvantu muṭikkum viṉimutaṟ peyarittōṟṟuvittu niṟkum ‘aṉ’ṉeṉṉum pāl kāṭṭum īṟu niṉṟu uṭimip poruḷit taṉakku urimiceyyum uṭimiyi virittu niṟkumeṉṟu uṇarka.
iṉi, ‘illattaṉ, puṟattaṉ’ eṉavum, ‘poṉṉaṉṉaṉ, puli pōl vāṉ’ eṉavum, ‘kariyaṉ, ceyyaṉ’ eṉavum varum. ‘kariyaṉeṉpatu, ‘ivvaṇṇamāy iruppaṉeṉṉum poruḷ uṇartti mutaṉiliyāy niṟṟaliṟ, paṇpām. ‘karumiya ṉeṉpatu, paṇpuppeyar uṭimipporuḷāy niṉṟu ‘karumiyiyuṭiyaṉ’ eṉa urupēṟṟu niṟṟaliṉ, uṭimip poruḷām. ‘ivvāṟaṉ uṭimipporuṭku iraṇṭāvatu viriyumāṟeṉṉiyeṉiṉ, ‘vaṉintāṉ’ eṉṉun terinili muṟṟuccol ‘ceytāṉeṉṉuṅ kāriyattiṉi nikaḻttum kāraṇaṅkaḷ eṭṭiṉiyum uḷḷaṭakki viṉimutalōṭu amintu māṟiniṉṟu avāynili tōṉṟiya kālattuc ceyappaṭuporuḷ mutaliya ēḻiṉiyum virittu niṟkumāṟu pōla, ‘uṭiyaṉeṉṉum viṉikkuṟippu muṟṟuccollum, uṭiyaṉāyiruntāṉ’ eṉa virintuḻi, uṇarappaṭuṅ kāriyat tiṉi nikaḻttum kāraṇaṅkaḷ eṭṭiṉiyum uḷḷaṭakki viṉimutalōṭu amintu māṟi niṉṟu avāynili tōṉṟiya kālattuc ceyappaṭuporuḷ mutaliya ēḻiṉiyum virittuniṟkum ātalāṉ, ‘uṭiyaṉeṉpataṉuḷ aṭaṅki niṉṟa ceyappaṭuporuḷ ‘kuḻiyi’ eṉa urupi virittal ataṟku iyalpeṉṟu uṇarka. iṭteriniliyum kuṟippum iṅṅaṉam urupi virittu niṟṟaliṉaṉṟē āciriyar, ‘aiyeṉap peyariya vēṟṟumik kiḷavi - yevvaḻi variṉum viṉiyē viṉik kuṟippavviru mutaliṟ ṟōṉṟumatuvē’ eṉṟatu.
teriniliviṉit toḻiṟpeyar kālan tōṉṟi niṉṟāṟpōlak ‘kacciṉāṉ, illattāṉ’ eṉṟāṟpōluṅ kuṟippuppeyaruṅ kālan tōṉṟiniṟkumeṉṟuṇarka.
‘taṉṉiṉam muṭittaleṉpataṉāl, ‘aiyāṭṭiyaṉ, tuṇaṅkiyaṉ’ eṉac ciṟupāṉmi kālamum viṉiceyyiṭamum paṟṟi varuvaṉavuṅ koḷka.
‘oppiṉāṉum’ eṉpataṟku utāraṇamākiya ‘poṉ ṉaṉṉāṉeṉpatu, oppumik kuṇappaṇpātaliṉ, viṉik kuṟippiṟkum kuṇappaṇpiṟkum uriyavāka mēṟkūṟukiṉṟa ‘vaṉmi’ mutaliyavaṟṟōṭu vikka.’ eṉiṉ, ik kuṇam uvamikkum uvamikkum poruṭkum oppaniṟṟaliṉ īṇṭu vittār, avaṟṟiṉ vēṟeṉṟu karuti.	(16)
216.	aṉmiyi ṉiṉmiyi ṉuṇmiyiṉ vaṉmiyi
	ṉaṉṉa piṟavuṅ kuṟippoṭu koḷḷu
	meṉṉa kiḷaviyuṅ kuṟippē kālam.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) aṉmiyiṉ iṉmiyiṉ uṇmiyiṉ 
vaṉmiyiṉ - aṉmi iṉmi uṇmi vaṉmi eṉṉum poruḷpaṟṟi varuvaṉavum, aṉṉa piṟavuṅ kuṟippoṭu koḷḷum eṉṉa kiḷaviyum - avi pōlvaṉa piṟavumāyk kuṟippup poruṇmi yoṭu poruntum ellāc collum, kālaṅ kuṟippu - kālaṅ kuṟippāṉ uṇarappaṭum collām, e-ṟu.
paṇpāvatu, oru poruḷ tōṉṟuṅ kālattu uṭaṉṟōṉṟi atu keṭuntuṇiyum niṟpatu. kuṟippāvatu, poruṭkuppiṉ ṟōṉṟic ciṟitu poḻutu nikaḻvatu. aṉmiyāvatu, ‘epporuḷumallaṉiṟivaṉ,’ eṉṟāṟ paṇpi uṇarttiyum, ‘avaṉtāṉ ivaṉ allaṉ,’ eṉṟāṟ kuṟippi uṇarttiyum niṟkum. itaṟku uṭampāṭu ‘āvaṉ’ eṉpatām. iṉi, uṇmikku iṉmi maṟi eṉṟu uṇarka. uṇmitāṉ mūvakiya: avi, ‘āvuṇṭeṉa uṇmiyi uṇarttiyum, ‘evvuyirk kaṇṇum iṟivaṉuḷaṉ’ eṉap paṇpi uṇarttiyum, ‘āṟṟarun tuppiṉ māṟṟōr pācaṟiuḷaṉ.’ eṉak kuṟippi uṇarttiyum niṟkum. iṉmiyāvatu, ‘poyyar neñciṟ puṉitayilaṉ,’ eṉap paṇpi uṇarttiyum. ‘māṟṟōr pācaṟi yilaṉ’ eṉak kuṟippi uṇarttiyum niṟkum. ivviṉmitāṉ, ‘kuḻi yilaṉ; kacci yilaṉ’ eṉa uṭimikku maṟiyāyum niṟkum. ivaṟṟiṟkup poruḷ urikkuṅkāṟ ‘kuḻiyi uṭiyaṉ allaṉ’, eṉa aṉmi varumāṟum uṇarka. ‘vaṉmi’ eṉṟatu, valiyum oṉṟaṉi vallutalumām. atu ‘mey vali yaṉṉeṉap paṇpi uṇarttiyuñ colal vallaṉeṉak kuṟippi uṇarttiyum niṟkum. itaṟku maṟi, ‘vallaṉalaṉeṉpatām. ‘allaḷ, allar, illaḷ, illar, uḷaḷ, uḷar, vallaḷ, vallar’ eṉavum oṭṭuka. ivvaṉmi mutaliya nāṉkaṉatu paṇpiyuṅ kuṟippiyum īṇṭuṅ koḷka.
‘aṉṉa piṟavum’ eṉṟataṉāl, ‘nallaṉ, nallaḷ, nallar, tīyaṉ, tīyaḷ, tīyar, uṭiyaṉ, uṭiyaḷ, uṭiyar eṉṉun toṭakkattaṉavuṅ koḷka.
ivviraṇṭu cūttirattāṟ kūṟiyavaṟṟi ‘am ām’ eṉpaṉa mutaliyavaṟṟuḷ ēṟpaṉavaṟṟoṭu paṭuttu oṭṭikkoḷḷumāṟu mēṟkūṟupa. (17)
217.	paṉmiyu morumiyum pālaṟi vanta
	vaṉṉa marapiṟ kuṟippoṭu varūuṅ
	kālak kiḷavi yuyartiṇi maruṅkiṉ
	mēlik kiḷaviyoṭu vēṟupā ṭilavē.
itu, muṟkūṟiya viṉikkuṟippiṟku īṟāmāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) paṉmiyum orumiyum pāl aṟivanta - paṉmiyum orumiyumākiya pālkaḷi aṟiya vanta, aṉṉa marapiṟ kuṟippoṭu varūum kālakkiḷavi - attaṉmittākiya muṟimiyiṉiyuṭiya kālaṅ kuṟittuk kōṭaloṭu varum viṉiccoṟkaḷ, uyartiṇi maruṅkiṉ mēlik kiḷavi yoṭu vēṟupāṭu ila - uyartiṇiyiṭattu mēṟkūṟiya terinili viṉiyiṉ īṟukaḷōṭu īṟu vēṟupāṭiṉṟit tamakku ēṟpaṉavaṟṟik koḷḷum, e-ṟu.
avi ‘am, ām, em, ēm, eṉ, ēṉ’ eṉṉun taṉmi īṟu āṟum, aṉ, āṉ, aḷ, āḷ, ar, ār’ eṉṉum paṭarkki yīṟu āṟumām. kariyam, kariyām; kariyem, kariyēm; kariyeṉ, kariyēṉ eṉavum, kariyaṉ kariyāṉ; kariyaḷ, kariyāḷ; kariyar, kariyār eṉavum varum. ‘uṭiyam, nilattam, poṉṉaṉṉam, allam’ eṉa oḻintavaṟṟōṭum oṭṭuka.	(18)
218.	aā va eṉa varūu miṟuti
	yappāṉ mūṉṟē palavaṟṟup paṭarkki.
itu, muṟiyāṉē aḵṟiṇit teriniliviṉi kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) a ā va eṉa varūum iṟuti appāl mūṉṟē - akaramum ākāramum vakara uyirmeyyumākiya īṟṟiyuṭiya akkūṟṟu mūṉṟu collum, palavaṟṟup paṭarkki - aḵṟiṇip paṉmip paṭarkki viṉiyām, e-ṟu.
akaram iṟantakālampaṟṟi varuṅkāl ‘ka, ṭa, ta, ṟa’ eṉṉum annāṉkaṉmuṉ ‘aṉ’ peṟṟum peṟātum, ēṉi yeḻuttiṉmuṉ ṅakāra ḻakāram oḻittu ‘iṉ’ peṟṟum, yakarattiṉmuṉ ciṟupāṉmi iṉṉēyaṉṟi ‘aṉ’ṉum peṟṟum peṟātum varum.
(u-m.) tokkaṉa, tokka, tokkila; uṇṭaṉa, uṇṭa, uṇṭila; vantaṉa, vanta, vantila; ceṉṟaṉa, ceṉṟa, ceṉṟila eṉavum; añciṉa, tappiṉa eṉavum, uriñiṉa, naṇṇiṉa, poruniṉa, cerumiṉa, tuṉṉiṉa eṉavum, pōyiṉa, cēriṉa, colliṉa, mēviṉa, tuḷḷiṉa eṉavum, pōyaṉa, pōya eṉavum varum.
iṉi, akaram nikaḻkālam paṟṟi varuṅkāl ‘nil, kiṉṟu’ eṉpaṉavaṟṟōṭu ‘aṉ’ peṟṟum peṟātum varum. uṇṇāniṉṟaṉa, uṇṇāniṉṟa, uṇṇāniṉṟila; uṇkiṉṟaṉa, uṇkiṉṟa, uṇkiṉṟila eṉa varum.
iṉi, akaram etirkālampaṟṟi varuṅkāṟ pakara vakarattōṭu ‘aṉ’ peṟṟum peṟātum varum. uṇpaṉa, uṇpa, uṇṇala; varuvaṉa, varuva eṉa varum. uriñuvaṉa, uriñuva eṉa ukaram peṟṟu ēṉi eḻuttiṉkaṇ varutalum ēṟpuḻik koḷka.
iṉi, ākāram kāleḻuttup peṟātu etirkālattup payiṉṟu maṟiyāy varum. uṇṇā, tiṉṉā eṉa varum.
iṉi, vakaram etirkālattiṟku urittāyk kukaram peṟṟum peṟātum, ēṟṟavaḻi ukaram peṟṟum varum. uṇkuva, tiṉkuva uṇṇala eṉavum, ōṭuva, tāvuva eṉavum, uriñuva, cerumuva eṉavum varum. uṇṭaṉavalla, uṇṭaṉavilli eṉap piṟa vāypāṭṭu maṟiyum aṟika.	(19)
219.	oṉṟaṉ paṭarkki ta ṟa ṭa vūrnta
	kuṉṟiya lukarat tiṟuti yākum.
iḵtu, orumi viṉi kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) oṉṟaṉ paṭarkki - oṉṟaṉi yuṇarttum paṭarkki viṉiyāvatu, ta ṟa ṭa ūrnta kuṉṟiyalukarattu iṟuti ākum - ta, ṟa, ṭa eṉṉum oṟṟukkaḷi ūrntu niṉṟa kuṉṟiya lukarattiṉi īṟākavuṭiya collām, e-ṟu.
takara ukaram iṟantakālattu varuṅkāl ‘ka, ṭa, ta, ṟa’ kkaḷ muṉ akaram peṟṟum, ēṉi yeḻuttiṉmuṉ ikaramum yakara uyirmeyyum peṟṟum, yakara uyirmeyyē peṟṟum varum.
(u-m.) pukkatu, pukkilatu; uṇṭatu, uṇṭilatu; vantatu, vantilatu; ceṉṟatu, ceṉṟilatu eṉavum, eñciyatu, tappiyatu eṉavum, uriñiyatu, naṇṇiyatu, poruniyatu, cerumiyatu, tuṉṉiyatu eṉavum, pōyatu, kūṟiyatu, colliyatu, mēviyatu, tuḷḷiyatu eṉavum varum.
iṉi, nil, kiṉṟu eṉpaṉavaṟṟōṭu nikaḻkālattiṉkaṇ akaram peṟṟu varum. naṭavāniṉṟatu, naṭavāniṉṟilatu; naṭakkiṉṟatu, naṭakkiṉṟilatu; uṇṇāniṉṟatu, uṇṇā niṉṟilatu; uṇkiṉṟatu, uṇkiṉṟilatu eṉa varum.
iṉi, etirkālattiṉkaṇ uyirmeyyāṉa pakara vakaram peṟṟu, uṇpatu, celvatu eṉa varum.
ṟakara ukaram, ‘ka, ṭa, ta, ṟa’ eṉpaṉavaṟṟiṉmuṉ aṉṉum iṉṉum peṟṟum, ēṉiyeḻuttiṉmuṉ ‘iṉ’ peṟṟum, avviṉṉiṉ ṉakaram tirintum iṟantakālam paṟṟi varum. pukkaṉṟu, pukkiṉṟu; uṇṭaṉṟu, uṇṭiṉṟu; vantaṉṟu, vantiṉṟu; ceṉṟaṉṟu, ceṉṟiṉṟu eṉa varum. ‘kūyiṉṟu, kūyiṟṟu, pōyiṉṟu, pōyiṟṟu’ eṉa ēṉi eḻuttōṭum oṭṭikkoḷka. ‘vantiṉṟu’ eṉpatu illiṉatu lakaram ṉakaramāyt tirinta etirmaṟi viṉiyātal, vantila, vantilaṉ, vantilaḷ, vantilar eṉṉum ēṉippāṟ collāṉ aṟika. inta ṟakara ukaram, ‘tāli kaḷintaṉṟu milaṉē’ eṉavum, ‘viyantaṉṟu miḻintaṉṟu milaṉē’eṉavum uyartiṇik kaṇṇum vantatāleṉiṉ, avi ‘kaḷintāṉ, viyantāṉ, iḻintāṉ’ eṉpaṉavaṟṟiṟku maṟiyāyk ‘kaḷintilaṉ, viyantilaṉ, iḻintilaṉ’ eṉa niṟkiṉṟavi ‘kaḷintaṉṟumilaṉ, viyantaṉṟumilaṉ, iḻintaṉṟumilaṉ’ eṉa muṟṟu viṉit tiricollāy niṉṟaṉa.
iṉi, ṭakara ukaram mūṉṟu kālattiṟkum uriya viṉikkuṟippāyk kuṇṭukaṭṭu, kuṟuntāṭṭu eṉa varum.
mēl kuṟippiṉit teriniliviṉiyōṭu māṭṭeṟi mātaliṉ, itteriniliviṉiyuḷ ṭakara ukara īṟu illatu kaṇṭu āṇṭu māṭṭēṟu ēlāteṉa īṇṭuk kūṟiṉāreṉa uṇarka.
kiḷaviyākkattut tiṇiyum pālum aṟivippāṉ ivvīṟukaḷi vitantōtiṉār; īṇṭu ivi iṉṉa iṭampaṟṟi uṇarttumeṉṟār.	(20)
220.	paṉmiyu morumiyum pālaṟi vanta
	vammū viraṇṭu maḵṟiṇi yavvē.
itu, virintatu tokuttut tiṇikku urimi kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) paṉmiyum orumiyum pāl aṟivanta - paṉmiyum orumiyumākiya pāl aṟiya vanta, ammūviraṇṭum aḵṟiṇiyavvē - avṟu āṟīṟṟuc collum aḵṟiṇi yuṭiyaṉavām, e-ṟu.	(21)
221.	attiṇi maruṅki ṉirupāṟ kiḷavikku
	mokku meṉpa vevaṉeṉ viṉāvē.
iḵtu, aḵṟiṇi irupāṟkum uriyatōr viṉik kuṟippuk kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) evaṉ eṉ viṉā - ‘evaṉeṉṉum viṉāccol attiṇi maruṅkiṉ irupāṟkiḷavikkum okkum eṉpa - aḵṟiṇiyiṭattu orumi paṉmiyākiya irupāṟ poruṇmikkum mikuti kuṟiviṉṟi okkum eṉṟu kūṟuvar āciriyar, e-ṟu.
(u-m.) aḵtevaṉ? avi yevaṉ? eṉa varum. ṉakara īṟāy irupāṟkum potuvāy varutaliṉ, vēṟē kūṟiṉār. numakku ivaṉ evaṉām? eṉpatu, muṟipaṟṟi niṟṟaliṉ, aḵṟiṇikkaṇ vantatām. paṭuttalōciyāl ‘evaṉeṉpatu peyarumām. aḵtu ik kālattu ‘eṉṉeṉṟum, ‘eṉṉiyeṉṟum maruviṟṟu. 	(22)

222.	iṉṟila vuṭiya veṉṉuṅ kiḷaviyu
	maṉṟuṭit talla veṉṉuṅ kiḷaviyum
	paṇpukoḷ kiḷaviyu muḷaveṉ kiḷaviyum
	paṇpi ṉākiya ciṉimutaṟ kiḷaviyu
	moppoṭu varūuṅ kiḷaviyoṭu tokii
	yappāṟ pattuṅ kuṟippoṭu koḷḷum.
iḵtu, aḵṟiṇi viṉikkuṟippuk kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) iṉṟu ila uṭiya eṉṉuṅ kiḷaviyum - iṉṟu, ila, uṭiya eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷum, aṉṟu uṭittu alla eṉṉum kiḷaviyum - aṉṟu, uṭittu, alla eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷum, paṇpu koḷ kiḷaviyum - niṟap paṇpit taṉakku aṭiyākakkoṇṭa collum, uḷa eṉ kiḷavi yum - uḷa eṉṉuñ collum, paṇpiṉ ākiya ciṉimutaṟ kiḷaviyum - niṟappaṇpāluṅ kuṇappaṇpālum uḷatākiya ciṉiyoṭu mutali uṇarttuñ collum, oppoṭu varūuṅ kiḷavi yoṭu tokii - uvamap poruḷpaṟṟi varuñ collōṭē tokku, appāṟ pattum kuṟippoṭu koḷḷum - akkūṟṟup pattuc colluṅ kālaṅ kuṟittukkōṭaloṭu varum viṉiccollākak koḷḷappaṭum, e-ṟu.
(u-m.) iṉṟu, ila; uṭittu, uṭiya; aṉṟu, alla; uḷa eṉpaṉa orumiyum paṉmiyumākiya vācakatti uṇartti niṉṟaṉa. allaṉa poruḷuṇartti niṉṟaṉa. ‘uṇṭeṉṉuñ col uṇmiyi uṇarttuṅkāl ‘iṉṟeṉṉuñ collum ataṟku maṟiyāy, ‘muyaṟkuk kōṭiṉṟeṉap poymmi yorumi uṇarttiyum, av ‘uṇṭeṉṉuñ  coṟ paṇpi uṇarttuṅkāl av ‘iṉṟeṉṉuñ collum ataṟku maṟiyāy, ikkutirikkekkālamum naṭiyiṉṟeṉap paṇpi ‘uṇarttiyum, av uṇṭeṉṉuñ col kuṟippi uṇarttuṅkāl  iṉṟeṉṉuñ collum ataṟku maṟiyāykkutirikku yīṇṭu naṭiyiṉṟeṉak kuṟippi uṇarttiyum niṟkum.’ ‘poruṇmi cuṭṭal’ eṉpataṉāṉ uṇmi uṇarttilum, ‘uṇṭeṉ kiḷaviyuṇmi ceppiṉ eṉpataṉāṉ viṉikkuṟippum paṇpum uṇarttiṉamiyum peṟṟām.
iṉi, ‘uḷaveṉṉum paṉmikku ‘ila’ eṉṉum paṉmi maṟiyām. itaṟku ‘uḷateṉṉum orumi īṟu ciṟupāṉmi yātaliṟ, kūṟiṟṟilar. atutāṉ, ‘māvuḷa’ eṉap paṇpu uṇarttiyum, ‘īṇṭu nelluḷa,’ eṉak kuṟippu uṇarttiyum niṟkum. iṉi, ‘aṟam ceyvārkkut tuṉpaṅkaḷila,’ eṉap paṇpu uṇarttiyum ‘īṇṭu uḻuntila,’ eṉak kuṟippu uṇarttiyum niṟkum.
iṉi, ‘uṭittu, uṭiya’ eṉṉum orumiyum paṉmiyum perumpāṉmi uṟuppiṉkiḻamiyum piṟitiṉkiḻamiyum paṟṟi varum. ivverutu kōṭuuṭittu; ivverutukaḷ kōṭuṭiya eṉap paṇpu uṇarttiyuṅ, ‘kuruti paṭintuṇṭa kākaṅ kukkiṟpuṟamuṭittu, uṭiya eṉak kuṟippu uṇarttiyum niṟkum. iṉṟu, ilaveṉpaṉa, ivvuṭimikku maṟutaliyāyk ‘kōṭiṉṟu; kōṭila,’ eṉa niṟkum. ivaṟṟiṟkup poruḷ urikkuṅkāṟkōṭuṭittaṉṟu; kōṭuṭiyatalla,’ eṉa ‘aṉṟu, alla’ eṉṉum vācaka varumāṟum uṇarka.
iṉi, ‘aṉṟu, alla’ eṉpaṉa orumiyum paṉmiyum uṇarttum. ‘uḻuntaṉṟu payaṟu; uḻuntalla payaṟu,’ eṉap paṇpu uṇarttiyum, ‘vēlaṉṟu veṉṟi taruvatu’ eṉavum, ‘paṭiyalla veṉṟi taruvaṉa,’ eṉavuṅ kuṟippu uṇarttiyum niṟkum.
‘oṉṟeṉa muṭittalāṉ, ‘naṉṟu, tītu; nalla, tīya’ eṉpaṉavuṅ koḷka.
paṇpu koḷ kiḷavi: karitu, kariya. paṇpiṉ ākiya ciṉi mutaṟ kiḷavi: veṇkōṭṭatu, veṇkōṭṭaṉa; neṭuñ cevittu, neṭuñ ceviya. oppoṭu varūuṅ kiḷavi: poṉṉaṉṉatu, poṉṉaṉṉa eṉa varum.
iṉi, ‘aṟinta mākkaṭ ṭākuka tilla’ ‘melviraṉ manti kuṟi kūṟum cemmaṟṟē’ mēṟṟu, vikaṟṟu. ‘aṇittō cēyttō kūṟumiṉ emakkē’ eṉpaṉa piṟitiṉkiḻamiyākiya uṭimipporuḷ paṭa niṉṟaṉa. ‘vaṭāatu teṉāatu’ eṉpaṉa ēḻaṉurupiṉ poruḷpaṭa vantaṉa. mūvāṭṭiyatu, celaviṟṟu eṉpaṉa kālamun toḻilumpaṟṟi vantaṉa. ivi ellām uriyiṟ kōṭalām.	(23)
223.	paṉmiyu morumiyum pālaṟi vanta
	vaṉṉa marapiṟ kuṟippoṭu varūuṅ
	kālak kiḷavi yaḵṟiṇi maruṅkiṉ
	mēlik kiḷaviyoṭu vēṟupā ṭilavē.
iḵtu, avviṉikkuṟippiṟku īṟāmāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) paṉmiyum orumiyum pāl aṟivanta - paṉmiyum orumiyumākiya pālkaḷi aṟiya vanta, aṉṉa marapiṟ kuṟippoṭu varūum kālak kiḷavi - attaṉmittākiya muṟimiyiṉiyuṭiya kālaṅ kuṟittukkōṭaloṭu varum viṉiccoṟkaḷ, aḵṟiṇi maruṅkiṉ mēlik kiḷaviyoṭu vēṟupāṭu ilavē - aḵṟiṇiyiṭattu mēṟkūṟiya teriniliviṉiyiṉ īṟukaḷōṭu īṟu vēṟupāṭu ila, e-ṟu.
akaramun takara ṟakara ukaramum perumpāṉmi avvīṟu paṟṟip pālum iṭamum uṇarttutal muṟkāṭṭiyavaṟṟuṭ kāṇka.
iṉi ākāram ‘immaṇi nalla, eṉṉum uṭampāṭṭuk kuṟippiṟku immaṇi pollā, eṉa varum.
iṉi, vakaraṅ, ‘katavavāṟ ṟakkavō kāḻkoṇṭa vaṉamuli? eṉpuḻik katattiṉiyuṭiya eṉa varum.
iṉit teriniliyuṭaṉ kūṭiya ṭakara ukaram pūṇi yuṭitteṉṉum poruṇmikkaṇ pūṭṭu eṉavum; iṭattatu eṉṉum poruṇmikkaṇ, ‘vakiterivāṉ kaṭṭē yulaku’ eṉavum; ennāḷateṉṉum poruṇmikkaṇ, ennāṭ ṭākunum pōrē? eṉavum; viḷiviṉiyuṭitte eṉṉum poruṇmikkaṇ, ‘vēli, āyiram viḷiyuṭṭāka eṉavum; kuḻiviṉiyuṭiya kaṇṇi yuṭitteṉṉum poruṇmik kaṭ, kuṇṭu kaṭṭu eṉavum; poruḷiṭam kālam toḻiluṟuppeṉṉum aivakippeyarum aṭiyāka varum. ōṭa vaṟṟu, ōṭa valla; ōṭa vallātu, ōṭa vallā eṉpaṉavuṅ koḷka.	(24)
224.	muṉṉili viyaṅkōḷ viṉiyeñcu kiḷavi
	yiṉmi ceppal vēṟeṉ kiḷavi
	ceymmaṉa ceyyuñ ceyta veṉṉu
	mammuṟi niṉṟa vāyeṇ kiḷaviyum
	piripuvēṟu paṭūuñ ceytiya vāki
	yirutiṇic coṟkumō raṉṉa vurimiya.
itu, niṟuttamuṟiyāṉē viravuviṉi uṇarttu kiṉṟavaṟṟiṉ peyarum muṟiyun tokiyuṅ kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) muṉṉili - etirmukamāka niṉṟa poruḷiṉatu toḻiluṇarttum muṟṟuccollum, viyaṅkōḷ - paṭarkkip poruḷmēl ēvaṟporuṇmiyik koṇṭuvarum muṟṟuc collum, viṉi eñcu kiḷavi - muṭikkum viṉiyi oḻipāka uṭiya viṉiccollum, iṉmi ceppal - iṉmi eṉṉum paṇpiṉi uṇarttum illi eṉṉum viṉikkuṟippu muṟṟuc collum, vēṟu eṉ kiḷavi - vēṟu eṉṉum vēṟupāṭutaṉṉi uṇarttiniṟkum viṉikkuṟippu muṟṟuc collum, ceymmaṉa - maṉa īṟṟup peyar eccamāy etirkālam uṇarttum ceymmaṉa eṉṉuñ collum, ceyyum - muṟṟum eccamumāy um īṟṟāl nikaḻkālam uṇarttuñ ceyyum eṉṉuñ collum, ceyta - akara īṟṟu eccamāy iṟanta kālam uṇarttum ceyta eṉṉuñ collum, eṉṉum ammuṟi niṉṟa a eṇ kiḷaviyum - eṉṟu collappaṭukiṉṟa avvaṭiviṉkaṇ niṉṟa avveṭṭuc collum, piripu vēṟupaṭūum ceytiya āki - potumiyiṟ pirintu orukāl uyartiṇiyiyum orukāl aḵṟiṇiyiyum uṇartti vēṟupaṭun toḻili uṭiyavāy, irutiṇic coṟkumōraṉṉa urimiya - irutiṇiyi uṇarttuñ collātaṟkum otta urimiyavām, e-ṟu.
eṉ ceyyāniṉṟāṉeṉṟu viṉāyavaḻi, uṇṇā niṉṟāṉeṉac cepputaliṉ, ceyyeṉṉum vācakam ellāt toḻiliṉiyum akappaṭutti  niṟṟalpeṟṟām. irutiṇikkum potuvāyatōr poruḷiṉmiyāṉ, ataṉāṉ nikaḻum viravuttoḻileṉpatu oṉṟiṉṟā leṉiṉ, avviravut toḻilik kūṟukiṉṟavaṉ ataṉi nikaḻttiya viṉimutal uyartiṇi aḵṟiṇiyeṉpatu uṇarntē kūṟumāyiṉum, pālkāṭṭum īṟiṉmi kāraṇat tāṟ kēṭṭōr uṇarvu irutiṇi viṉimutaṉmēlum oruṅku cēṟalāṉ, avviṉimutaṟ potumiyāṉ nikaḻum viravut toḻilum uḷavāyiṉa.	(25)

225.	avaṟṟuḷ,
	muṉṉilik kiḷavi
	iai āyeṉa varūu mūṉṟu
	moppat tōṉṟu moruvaṟku moṉṟaṟkum.
itu muṟiyāṉē muṉṉiliyorumiviṉi kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) avaṟṟuḷ muṉṉilik kiḷavi - avveṭṭaṉuḷ muṉṉilic col i ai āy eṉa varūum mūṉṟum - ‘i, ai, āy’ eṉṟu colla varukiṉṟa mūṉṟu īṟṟuccollum, oruvarkkum oṉṟaṟkum oppat tōṉṟum - uyartiṇi yāṇpālum peṇpālumākiya orumippāṟkum aḵṟiṇi orumippāṟkum mikuti kuṟiviṉṟi oppat tōṉṟum, e-ṟu.
ikaram, ṭa, ta, ṟa ūrntu etirkālam paṟṟi varum.
(u-m.) uṇṭi, uritti, tiṉṟi. ivaṟṟi uṇpi, urippi, tiṉpi eṉa virikka. iṉi, urikkiṟṟi, ‘naṉṟumaṉ  nāṭāy kūṟi’ eṉa nikaḻvu paṟṟiyum varum. ivitām eṭuttal ōciyāṟ kūṟumvaḻi, uṇpāy, urippāy, tiṉpāy eṉa ēvaṟporuṇmiyum uṇarttum. iṉi, ‘aiya! ciṟiteṉṉi yūkki’eṉavum, ‘ītaṉmāṭ ṭotti peruma!’ eṉavuṅ kakaramun takaramum peṟṟa ikaram ēval kaṇṇiyē niṟkum. itaṟkup ‘pirintuṟi cūḻāti’ eṉpatu maṟi.
iṉi, aikāram am īṟṟukku uriya eḻuttup peṟṟum, āyīṟu āmīṟṟukku uriya eḻuttup peṟṟum, mūṉṟu kālattum varum. uṇṭaṉi, uṇṇāniṉṟaṉi, uṇpi, uṇkuvi, kariyi eṉavum; uṇṭāy, uṇṇāniṉṟāy, uṇpāy, kariyāy eṉavum varum. uriñiṉi. uriñiṉāy eṉa ēṉi eḻuttōṭum oṭṭuka uri eṉṟāl, ēval kaṇṇumāṟum uṇarka. uṇṇāniṟṟi eṉpatu, etirkālattup piṟanta nikaḻkālam. iṉi, uṇṭili uṇṇāniṉṟili, uṇṇali eṉavum, uṇṭilāy, uṇṇāniṉṟilāy, uṇṇalāy eṉavum maṟi varum. 	(26)
226.	irīr miṉṉeṉa varūu mūṉṟum
	pallōr maruṅkiṉum palavaṟṟu maruṅkiṉuñ
	collō raṉiya veṉmaṉār pulavar.
itu, muṉṉilip paṉmi kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) ir īr miṉ eṉa varūum mūṉṟum - ir, īr, miṉ eṉṟu colla varukiṉṟa mūṉṟu īṟṟuc collum, pallōr maruṅkiṉum pala vaṟṟu maruṅkiṉum - uyartiṇikkaṇ pallōriṭattiṉum aḵṟiṇik kaṇ palavaṟṟiṭattiṉum, col ōr aṉiya eṉmaṉār pulavar - collutali orutaṉmiyāka uṭiya eṉṟu colluvar āciriyar, e-ṟu.
ir īṟu ar īṟṟukku uriya eḻuttum, īr īṟu ār īṟṟukku uriya eḻuttum peṟṟu mūṉṟukālattum varum.
(u-m.) uṇṭaṉir, uṇṇāniṉṟaṉir, uṇpir, uṇkuvir eṉavum, uṇṭīr, uṇṇāniṉṟīr, uṇpīr, uṇkuvīr eṉavum varum. miṉ piṟa eḻuttup peṟātu, ēṟṟuḻi ukarampeṟṟu etirkālattu varum. ‘uṇmiṉ kaḷḷē, aṭumiṉ cōṟē’ uriñumiṉ eṉa varum. itaṟku, uṇpīr,aṭuvīr, uriñuvīr eṉa virikka. ‘kāṉam kaṭattir eṉak kēṭpiṉ’ eṉpuḻik kaṭavāniṉṟīreṉa nikaḻkālattut takaram vantatu. iḵtu etirkālattum varum.
iṉi, muṉṉili viṉikkuṟippu, uyartiṇi viṉikkuṟippiṟku ōtiya poruḷpaṟṟi aiyum āyum iruvum īrum eṉa nāṉku īṟṟavāy kaḻaliṉi, nāṭṭi, poṉṉaṉṉi, kariyi; kaḻaliṉāy, nāṭṭāy, poṉṉaṉṉāy, kariyāy; kaḻaliṉir, nāṭṭiṉir, poṉṉaṉṉir, kariyir; kaḻaliṉīr, nāṭṭiṉīr, poṉṉaṉṉīr, kariyīr eṉa varum. pōṟi eṉpatu pōṉṟaṉaṉ, pōṉṟāṉ eṉpatupōla vanta teriniliviṉi. iṉi, uṇṭilir, uṇṇāniṉṟilir, uṇṇalir; uṇṭilīr. uṇṇāniṉṟilīr, uṇṇalīr; uṇṇaṉmiṉ eṉa maṟi varum.
mēl eccaviyaluḷ ‘muṉṉili muṉṉar’ (451)  cūttirattāṟ peṟṟa muṉṉilikaḷuḷ moḻikku īṟāmeṉap paṭṭa naṭa, vā, viri, ī, koṭu, kū, mē, ki, no, pō, veḷa eṉṉum uyirīṟu patiṉoṉṟum, uriñ, maṇ, porun, tirum, tiṉ, tēy, pār, cel, tev (vav), tāḻ, koḷ eṉṉum puḷḷiyīṟu patiṉoṉṟum, pōkku, pāyccu, ūṭṭu, naṭattu, eḻuppu, tīṟṟu, veḵku eṉa eḻu vakiyavākiya kuṟṟiyalukara īṟu oṉṟumāka eḻuttuvaki irupattumūṉṟum eṭuttalōciyāṉ muṉṉili yēvalorumi muṟṟāy niṟkum. ivitāmē paṭuttalōci yāṉ ac ceykimēl peyarttaṉmi paṭa mutaṉiliyāyum niṟkum. aṅṅaṉam mutaṉiliyāy niṟkuṅkāl naṭantāṉ, vantāṉ, virintāṉ, īntāṉ, koṭuttāṉ, kūviṉāṉ, mēviṉāṉ, kittāṉ, nontāṉ, pōyiṉāṉ, veḷaviṉāṉ eṉa uyirīṟu patiṉoṉṟum; uriñiṉāṉ, maṇṇiṉāṉ, poruniṉāṉ, tirumiṉāṉ, tiṉṟāṉ, tēyttāṉ, pārttāṉ, ceṉṟāṉ, vavviṉāṉ, tāḻntāṉ, koṇṭāṉ eṉap puḷḷiṟu patiṉoṉṟum; pōkkiṉāṉ, pāycciṉāṉ, ūṭṭiṉāṉ, naṭattiṉāṉ, eḻuppiṉāṉ, tīṟṟiṉāṉ, veḵkiṉāṉeṉak kuṟṟiyalukara vīṟu oṉṟumāka muṟṟuccol virintu niṉṟavāṟu kāṇka. naṭantu vantāṉ, vantu pōṉāṉ, virintu kiṭantāṉ, īttu vantāṉ eṉavum; naṭanta cāttaṉ, vanta cāttaṉ, virinta cāttaṉ, ītta cāttaṉ eṉavum; uriñi vantāṉ, poruni vantāṉ eṉavum; uriñiṉa cāttaṉ, poruniṉa cāttaṉ eṉavum; viṉiyeccattiṟkum peyareccattiṟkum ivvāṟē oḻinta mutaṉilikaḷiyum virittukkoḷka.
muṟkāṭṭiya muṉṉili yēvalorumi muṟṟukkaḷ oruvaṉi nōkki, oṉṟaṉi oru toḻilic ceyviyeṉṉum poruṇmikkaṇ naṭattuvi, naṭappi, varuvi, virivi, virippi, īppi, koṭuppi, kūvuvi, mēvuvi, kippi, novvuvi, pōkkuvi, veḷavuvi, uriñuvi, maṇṇuvi, porunuvi, tirumuvi, tiṉpi, tēyppi, pārppi, celuttuvi, tevvuvi, tāḻttuvi, koḷḷuvi, veḵkuvi eṉa etirkālattiṟku uriya vakaramum pakaramum muṉṉilikku uriya ikaram ēṟi iṭinilikku uriya eḻuttukkaḷum peṟṟu eṭuttalōciyāṉ niṟkumeṉṟu uṇarka.
iṉṉum avitām, paṭarkkiyiṭattup pālkāṭṭum muṟṟāy, naṭattuvittāṉ, naṭappittāṉ, varuvittāṉ, virivittāṉ, virippittāṉ, īppittāṉ, koṭuppittāṉ, kūvuvittāṉ, mēvuvittāṉ, kippittāṉ, novvuvittāṉ, pōkkuvittāṉ, veḷavuvittāṉ, uriñuvittāṉ, maṇṇu vittāṉ, porunuvittāṉ, tirumuvittāṉ, tiṉpittāṉ, tēyppittāṉ, pārppittāṉ, celuttuvittāṉ, tevvu vittāṉ, tāḻttuvittāṉ, koḷvittāṉ, veḵkuvittāṉ eṉa varuṅkāl iṭiniliyākiya eḻuttukkaḷum peṟṟu muṉṉar eṭuttalōciyāy iṭiniṉṟa īṟākiya ikaram paṭuttalōcippaṭṭu iṭiniliyāy niṟku meṉṟu uṇarka.
iṉi, naṭattuvippi, varuvippi, naṭappippi eṉavum varumeṉṟu kūṟi, ivaṟṟi irumaṭi yēvalēṉpārum uḷar. ivaṟṟi irukālēvutal kūṟiyatu kūṟalāmātaliṉ, ivi iḻivaḻakkeṉa maṟukka. iṉi, uṇṇun tiṉṉumeṉap paṉmikkaṇ varum um īṟum, uṇṇaleṉa varum allīṟṟu maṟiyum, maṟāleṉa varum ālīṟum, ‘akiyēl mātōḻiyaḻēl,’ eṉa varum ēlīṟum, uṇṭukāṇ, collikkāṇ eṉa varum kāṇ īṟumākiya colvaki aintum muṉṉiṟ cūttirattu etirkālamoṉṟiṉum varum ikaram muṟkūṟiya muṟiyaṉṟik kūṟṟiṉāṟ koḷka.
muṟkūṟiya eḻuttuvaki irupattumūṉṟum colvaki aintumākiya irupatteṭṭum muṉṉiliyēvalorumi muṟṟeṉṟu uṇarka. naṭavāy, vārāy, uṇṇāy, tiṉṉāy eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷ, āyīṟu keṭṭu, naṭa, vā, uṇ, tiṉ eṉa niṟkumeṉṟu cēṉāvariyar kūṟiṉārā leṉiṉ, atu poruntāmi eccaviyaluṭ ceyyāyeṉṉum muṉṉili viṉiccol’ eṉṉuñ cūttirattuḷ kūṟutum.	(27)
227.	eñciya kiḷavi yiṭattoṭu civaṇi
	yimpāṟku muriya tōṉṟa lāṟē.
itu, muṉṉili yoḻintavaṟṟiṉ ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) eñciya kiḷavi - muṉṉili viṉiyoḻittu oḻinta ēḻu viṉiccollum, iṭattoṭu civaṇi aimpāṟkum uriya - mūṉṟiṭattoṭum porunti aintupāṟkum uriyavām, tōṉṟalāṟē - avi tattam poruṭkaṇ tōṉṟum iṭattu, e-ṟu.
mēl vilakkuvaṉa oḻintaṉa īṇṭuk kāṭṭutum: avaṉ celka, avaḷ celka, avar celka, atu celka, avi celka eṉavum; uḻutu vantēṉ. uḻutu vantēm; uḻutu vantāy, uḻutu vantīr; uṇṭu vantāṉ, uṇṭu vantāḷ, uṇṭu vantār, uḻutu vantatu, uḻutu vantaṉa eṉavum; yāṉ illi, yāṉum nīyumilli yāṉumavaṉumilli yāṉunīyumavaṉumilli nāmilli yāmilli nīyilli nīyirilli avaṉilli avaḷilli avarilli atuvilli aviyilli eṉavum, yāṉvēṟu yāṉunīyum vēṟu yāṉumavaṉumvēṟu yāṉunīyumavaṉumvēṟu nāmvēṟu yām vēṟu nīvēṟu nīyirvēṟu avaṉvēṟu avaḷvēṟu avarvēṟu atu vēṟu avivēṟu eṉavum, yāṉuṇmaṉa yāṉunīyumuṇmaṉa yāṉumavaṉumuṇmaṉa yāṉunīyumavaṉu muṇmaṉa nāmuṇmaṉa yāmuṇmaṉa nīyuṇmaṉa nīyiruṇmaṉa avaṉuṇmaṉa avaḷuṇmaṉa avaruṇmaṉa atuvuṇmaṉa aviyuṇmaṉa eṉavum. yāṉuṇṇumūṇ yāṉunīyu muṇṇumūṇ yāṉumavaṉumuṇṇumūṇ avaruṇṇu mūṇ atuvuṇṇumūṇ  aviyuṇṇumaṇ eṉavum, avaṉ varum avaḷ varum atuvarum avirum eṉavum, yāṉuṇṭavūṇ yāṉunīyumuṇṭavūṇ yāmuṇṭavūṇ nīyuṇṭavūṇ nīyīruṇṭa vūṇ avaṉuṇṭavūṇ avaḷuṇṭavūṇ avaruṇvūṇ atuvuṇṭavūṇ aviyuṇṭavūṇ eṉavum varum.
viyaṅkōḷum, maṉa īṟum etirkālam uṇarttum. iṉmi ceppalum, vēṟu eṉpatum mūṉṟu kālamum uṇarttum. uṇmaṉa eṉpatu, yāṉuṇṇum, nīyuṇṇum, avaṉuṇṇum eṉṉum poruṇmi uṇartti, peyarecca nīrmittāy, iṟanta vaḻakkiṟṟāy varum. icceyyumeṉṉum vācakattukku akkālattu maṉeṉṉum vācakattālum vaḻaṅku taliṉ, āciriyar maṉa eṉpataṉi īṇṭu vēṟāka ōti, eḻuttōtti ṉuḷḷuñceymmaṉa eṉṉun toḻiliṟu collum eṉṟār. yām nām eṉpataṉi iṉmi ceppaṉ mutaliyavaṟṟōṭum oṭṭuka.	(28)
228.	avaṟṟuḷ,
	muṉṉili taṉmi yāyī riṭattoṭu
	maṉṉā tākum viyaṅkōṭ kiḷavi.
iḵtu, eytiyatu vilakkiṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) avaṟṟuḷ - mēl eñciya kiḷaviyeṉṉappaṭṭa ēḻaṉuḷ, viyaṅkōṭkiḷavi - ēvaṟporuṇmiyi muṟṟa muṭittali vuṇarttuñ col, muṉṉili taṉmi āyīriṭattoṭum maṉṉātākum - muṉṉiliyun taṉmi yumākiya avviraṇṭiṭattup poruḷōṭum aḵṟiṇik kaṭ perumpāṉmiyum nilipeṟātu; uyartiṇikkaṭ perum pāṉmiyum nilipeṟum, e-ṟu.
eṉavē, paṭarkkip poruṭkaṇ ‘irutiṇic coṟkumōraṉṉarimiya’ ām. itaṟku utāraṇam muṉṉark kāṭṭiṉām.
‘nōyumiṉpamum’ eṉṉum poruḷiyaṟcūttirattiṟ ‘collā marapiṉavaṟṟoṭu keḻīic- ceyyā marapiṟ ṟoḻiṟpaṭuttaṭakkiyum’ eṉṟum, ‘ñāyiṟu tiṅkaḷ’ eṉṉuñ ceyyuḷiyaṟ cūttirattu, ‘colliyāṅ kamiyumeṉmaṉār pulavar’ eṉṟum aḵṟiṇi ēvaṟporuṇmiyi muṟṟa muṭiyāmiyik kūṟukiṉṟa āciriyar, avaṟṟiṟku ēṟpa īṇṭum muṉṉilikkaṇ varum aḵṟiṇi ēvaṟporuṇmiyi muṟṟa muṭiyātu eṉpatu uṇarttutaṟku ‘maṉṉātākum’ eṉṟār. eṉavē, ‘aḵṟiṇikkaṇ varum muṉṉili viyaṅkōṭku ōraṉṉa urimi iṉṟeṉṟārāyiṟṟu. taṉmi yaḵṟiṇikku iṉmi kūṟalvēṇṭāvāyiṉum, uyartiṇikkaṭ ciṟupāṉmi vaḻaṅkutalpaṟṟi uṭaṉōtiṉār.
muṟkūṟiya naṭa, vā mutaliya irupattumūṉṟu īṟṟu muṉṉiliyēvalorumiyum aḵṟiṇikkaṇ varuṅkāl ēvalviṉiyi uṇarntu avi muṟṟa muṭiyā eṉpataṉi uṇarntu ataṉi ēvutal marapaṉṟeṉṟu karuti āciriyar iṅṅaṉam ōtiṉār.
avitāṅ kūṟukiṉṟavar karuttāṉ, ēval kaṇṇiyē varum. uyarntāṉ iḻintāṉi iṉṉatu ceykaveṉa vitittal ēval kaṇṇiyatu. iḻintāṉ uyarntāṉi ‘iṉṉatu ceyyavēṇṭumeṉa vēṇṭik kōṭa lum ēval kaṇṇiṟṟu. iṉi, uyartiṇip poruḷākiya muṉṉilikkaṇ vāḻka, vāḻiya eṉa vāḻttup poruṇmikkaṇ vantatum ēval kaṇṇiṟṟu. ‘kaṭāvuka pāka! niṉ kālvaṉeṭuntēr’ eṉa vēṇṭik kōṭaṟ poruṇmikkaṇ vantatum ēval kaṇṇiṟṟu. iṉi, uyar tiṇit taṉmikkaṇ, ‘yāṉum niṉṉō ṭuṭaṉuṟika.’ eṉa vēṇṭik kōṭaṟ poruṇmik kaṇ vantatum ēval kaṇṇiṟṟu. itu ciṟupāṉmiyeṉpatu uṇarttutaṟku muṉṉiliyi muṟkūṟiṉār. iṉi, eḻuttōt tiṉuḷ, ‘ēval kaṇṇiya viyaṅkōṭ kiḷaviyum’  eṉa akara īṟṟuḷ eṭuttu ōtiṉamiyāṟ poruntiya meyyi ūrntu akara īṟāy varutalum; uṭampoṭu puṇarttaleṉpataṉāl, ‘vaḻāal ōmpal’ eṉavum, ‘iṟṟeṉak kiḷattal’ eṉavum, ‘ākka moṭu kūṟal’ eṉavum vanta al īṟum, ‘marīiya torāal’ eṉa varum ālīṟumāy varutalum, oṉṟeṉa muṭittaleṉpa taṉāṉ vāḻiyareṉṉum ar īṟumaṟikkaṭ ‘kāṇaṉmā remar’eṉṉum ār īṟum, ‘vāḻtal vēṇṭumivaṇ varinta vikal’eṉa ummīṟum, ‘añcāmiyañcuvatoṉṟiṉ’ eṉa aikāra īṟumāy varutaluṅ koḷka.
iṉṉum itaṉāṉē, ‘makaṉeṉal makkaṭ pataṭiyeṉal’ eṉa uṭampāṭum maṟiyumāy varutaluṅ koḷka.
iṉṉum itaṉāṉē, ‘nāṭākoṉṟō, kāṭā koṉṟō eṉpaṉavaṟṟiyum nāṭēyāka, kāṭēyāka eṉa itaṉpāṟpaṭuttuka. ivaṟṟuḷ ēval kaṇṇātatu ituveṉa uṇarka.	(29)
229.	pallōr paṭarkki muṉṉili taṉmi
	yavvayiṉ mūṉṟu nikaḻuṅ kālattuc
	ceyyu meṉṉuṅ kiḷaviyoṭu koḷḷā.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) pallōr paṭarkki muṉṉili taṉmi avvayiṉ mūṉ ṟum - pallōratu paṭarkkiyum muṉṉiliyum taṉmiyu mākiya av veñciya kiḷavikkaṇ varum mūṉṟum, nikaḻum kālattuc ceyyum eṉṉum kiḷaviyoṭu koḷḷā - nikaḻuṅ kālattu varuñceyyum eṉṉum muṟṟuc colloṭu poruntā, e-ṟu.
(u-m.) ōotal vēṇṭumoḷimāḻkuñ ceyviṉi, āatum eṉṉumavar.’ ‘nīyē eṉkuṟi collal vēṇṭumāl alava!’ ‘yāṉ porutal vēṇṭum’ eṉa varuvaṉavaṟṟiṟkut tavirvārāka eṉavum, colvāyāka eṉavum, poruvēṉāka eṉavum poruḷ urikka. 	(30)
230.	ceytu ceyyūc ceypu ceyteṉac
	ceyyiyar ceyyiya ceyiṉceyac ceyaṟkeṉa
	vavvaki oṉpatum viṉiyeñcu kiḷavi.
itu, muṟiyāṉē viṉiyeccam uṇarttu kiṉṟavaṟṟuḷ ciṟantaṉa tokuttu uṇarttukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) ceytu ceyyū ceypu ceyteṉa ceyyiyar ceyyiya ceyiṉ ceya ceyaṟku eṉa avvaki oṉpatum viṉiyeñcu kiḷavi - ceyteṉpatu mutalākac collappaṭṭa akkūṟṟu oṉpatu vāypāṭum muṟkūṟiya viṉiyeccamām, e-ṟu.
 itu, ‘tokutta moḻiyiṉ vakuttaṉar kōṭal.’ cey yeṉṉum vāypāṭṭiṉi ukaramum, ūkāramum, pukaramum, eṉavum, iyarum, iyavum, iṉṉum, akaramum kukaramum eṉṉum iṟuti iṭiccol lāṉ vēṟupaṭuttiṉār. ceytu eṉpatu iṟantakālam paṟṟi varuṅkāl kuṟṟukarattāṉ ūrappaṭṭa, ka, ṭa, ta, ṟa, eṉṉum nāṉkum, ikara īṟum, yakara īṟum eṉa aṟuvakittāmēṉum, ikara īṟum yakara īṟum ceytu eṉṉum vācakattit tantē niṟṟaliṟ, poruṇmiyāṉ oṉṟāka aṭakkappaṭṭaṉa.
(u-m.) nakku, uṇṭu, vantu, ceṉṟu; eñci, tappi, uriñi, ōṭi; āy, pōy eṉa varum.
ivaṟṟuḷ ukaram ikaramāyt tirintateṉiṉ, uriñi eṉpataṉi ‘ñakāriyoṟṟiya’ eṉṉuñ cūttirattāṉ uriñ eṉa niṟutti, nili moḻit toḻilākiya ukaram peṟumeṉa vitittaliṉ, uriñu eṉṉum muṟṟukaran tirintu uriñi eṉa niṉṟateṉṟal poruntāmi uṇarka.
iṉi, āku, pōku eṉak kuṟṟukara īṟṟāṉ mutaṉili yāyiṟṟeṉil, ākiṉāṉ, pōkiṉāṉ eṉa muṟṟum ataṉkaṇṇē tōṉṟutal vēṇṭum. aṅṅaṉam vaḻaṅkāmiyiṉ ā, pō eṉpaṉa mutalniliyāyk kālaṅ kāṭṭum yakara oṟṟup peṟṟu āyiṉāṉ, pōyiṉāṉ eṉa muṟṟum ataṉkaṇṇē tōṉṟutaliṉ āku, pōku eṉak kuṟṟukara īṟṟu mutaṉili iṉṟāmeṉṟu uṇarka. itaṉāṉē viṉiyeccam muṟṟāyt tiriyum eṉpatum uṇarka. avi tūyiṉāṉ, tāyiṉāṉ, mēyiṉāṉ, ēyiṉāṉ, ciṉiiyiṉāṉ, muṉiiyiṉāṉ, uriiyiṉāṉ, marīiyi ṉāṉ, uṭīiyiṉāṉ, kaṭiiyiṉāṉ, taḻīiyiṉāṉ, kuḻīiyiṉāṉ, parāyiṉāṉ. virāyiṉāṉ, nilāyiṉāṉ, kalāyiṉāṉ eṉa varum. ivi iṅṅaṉam muṟṟāyt tiritaliṉ, peyarkoṇṭu muṭivaṉa āyiṉa. āy, pōy eṉpaṉa, ‘viṉiyeñcu kiḷaviyum vēṟupal kuṟiya’ eṉpataṉāṟ tirintu niṟkumeṉpatu kōṭaliṉ, tirintu niṉṟaṉa. accūttirattu viṉiyeccam muṟṟuviṉi koḷḷumāṟuṅ kāṭṭutum. muṉṉark kāṭṭiya utāraṇaṅkaḷuḷ ukaram ikaramāyt tiriyāmiyum ivvāṟē pirittu uṇarntu koḷka.
‘viruntiṉṟi yuṇṭa pakalum’ eṉavum, ‘nāḷaṉṟu pōki’ eṉavum varuvaṉa ceyteṉ eccakkuṟippu. ceytu eṉpataṟku, ōṭāniṉṟu pōyiṉāṉ eṉavum, ‘akaliru vicumpiṟ pāyiruḷ parukip, pakalkāṉ ṟeḻutaru’ eṉavum, ‘talippeyaṟ karumpīṉṟu, muḷimutaṟ potuḷiya muṭpuṟap piṭavamum’ eṉavum nikaḻkāla vāypāṭum ciṟupāṉmi uḷateṉṟu uṇarka. nakku eṉpatu naku eṉṉum mutaṉilikkaṇ virinta nakutaleṉṉum viṉip peyaraṭiyākac ceyteṉṉum viṉiyeccan tōṉṟi, nakutalic ceyteṉṉum poruḷtantu niṉṟatu. iṉi, ūkāram, uṇṇū vantāṉ; eṉavum, ‘paṭumakaṉ kiṭakki kāṇūu’ eṉavum, ‘nilampuṭiyūveḻutarum valampaṭu kuñcaram’  eṉavum piṉvarum toḻiṟku iṭiyiṉṟi virivu uṇartti muṉvarun toḻiṉmēl iṟantakālampaṟṟi varum. ākāram uṇṇā eṉavum, ‘kallāk kaḻippar’eṉavum, ‘nilaṅkiḷiyā nāṇiniṉṟōḷ’ eṉavum varum. ākāram viṉiyeccamāy varutal, ‘ceyyā eṉṉum viṉiyeñcu kiḷaviyum, avviyal tiriyā teṉmaṉār pulavar’ eṉpataṉāṉ peṟappaṭum. iṉip pakara ukaram ‘palkālvākkuputarattara varuttamvīṭavāravuṇṭu’ eṉavum “pularāppacciliyiliyiṭiyiṭuputoṭutta” eṉavum perumpāṉmi iṟantakālattuvarum. appukaram nakupuvantāṉ eṉpuḻi nakā niṉṟuvantāṉeṉṟu muṭikkuñcollāl uṇarappaṭun toḻirōṭu uṭaṉikaḻntu nikaḻkālattum varum. atu “vāṭupuvaṉap pōṭi” eṉavum varum. uriñapu eṉa ukaramuṅ kaṟkupu eṉakkukaramum ēṟpuḻi varum. iṉi eṉa eṉpatu “ka ṭa ta ṟa ūrntu iṟantakālampaṟṟi muṭikkuñ collāl uṇarappaṭun toḻiṟkut taṉ mutaṉilittoḻil kāraṇamāya virivu poruḷuṇarttum. cōlipukkeṉaveppunīkkiṟṟu uṇṭeṉap pacikeṭṭatu urit teṉavuṇarntāṉ maruntutiṉṟeṉap piṇinīṅkiṟṟu eṉavarum. eñciyeṉa eṉavum uriñiyeṉa eṉavum eṉiyeḻuttōṭum oṭṭuka. iṉi iyar iya eṉpaṉa uṇṇiyar tiṉṉiyar eṉavum uṇṇiya tiṇṇiya eṉavum etirkālampaṟṟivarum. pōkiyar pōkiya eṉa ēṟpuḻik kakaramumpeṟum. iṉi iṉ etirkālam paṟṟik kāraṇap poruṭṭāy varum. maḻipeyyiṟkuḷaniṟiyum meyyuṇariṉ viṭeḷitām eṉavarum. naṭappiṉ urippiṉ eṉa ēṟpuḻip pakaramumpeṟum. itu “naṉaviṟpuṇarcci naṭakkalumāṅkē” eṉa ummīṟātalum “aṟṟālaḷavaṟintuṇka” eṉa ālīṟātalum “viṉi yeñcukiḷaviyum vēṟupalkuṟiya” eṉpataṉāṟ peṟappaṭum, “avāvuṇṭēluṇṭām” eṉṉum ēlum “avaḷeḷḷumēṉumvarum” eṉṉum ēṉum itaṉ kuṟippu. “oṉṟāṉuntīccol” eṉpuḻi āyiṉumeṉpatu āṉumeṉak kuṟintuniṉṟatu. “nuṇaṅkiyakēḷviyā lāleṉpataṉṟi” eṉṉuñ ceyteṉeccakkuṟippu uṇarttuva tōr vāypāṭu allāvāyiṉeṉṟu poruḷāyiṉ itaṉkuṟippām. iṉi akaram iṟantakālattukkaṇ maḻipeyyakkuḷaniṟintatu eṉak kāraṇap poruḷāyuṅ kuḷaniṟiyamaḻi peytatu eṉak kāriyap poruḷāyum varum. iṉi nikaḻkālattukkaṇ nāyiṟupaṭa vantā ṉeṉpatu nikaḻāniṟkavantāṉeṉṉum poruṭṭāy varum. “vāḻac ceyta nalviṉiyulakam” eṉpatum atu. iṉi etirkālattukkaṇ uṇṇavantāṉ eṉa ataṟporuṭṭāy varum. iṉit “tuṉṉip periyavōtiṉuñciṟiyavuṇarā” eṉa akaram viṉikkuṟippup paṟṟi varuvaṉavuñ “cevvaṉṟeri kiṟpāṉ” eṉavum “putuvatiṉiyaṉṟavaṇiyaṉ” eṉavum “putuvatupuṉintaveṇkiyāppa” eṉavum “poykip pūpputitīṉa” eṉavum “peruṅkiyaṟṟaveṉ pulampu” eṉavum “ciṟunaṉinī tuñciyēṟpiṉum” eṉavum “ollik koṇṭāṉ” eṉavum akara īṟaṉṟip piṟa īṟukaḷāṉ muṭikkuñ colli vicēṭittuvaruvaṉavum “viṉiyeñcukiḷaviyumvēṟupalkuṟiya” eṉpataṉāṟ peṟappaṭum. itu maṟikkaṭ “kūṟāmaṟ kuṟittataṉ mēṟ celluṅkaṭuṅkūḷi” eṉa allīṟāyum “kūṟāmi nākkiṟkuṟippaṟivāṉ” eṉa makara aikāra īṟāyum varum. iṉik kukaram uṇaṟkuvantāṉ eṉa etirkālattu varum. uṇṭāṉ uṇṭavaṉ eṉṉun toḻiṟpeyarkaḷ uṇṭāṟku uṇṭavaṟku eṉa nāṉkaṉurupipēṟṟuḻic ceyaṟku eṉum eccapporuḷ tarum mayakkamiṉmiyum uṇaveṉṉum viṉippeyar kukaram aṭuttuḻi uṇṭalicceytaṟkeṉṉum poruḷ tantu eccap poruṭṭāyē niṟṟaliṉ viṉippeyar nāṉkaṉurupu peṟṟu niṉṟateṉṉum mayakkam itaṟku iṉmiyum uṇarka. eṟṟukkuvantāṉeṉpatu ekkāriyañceytaṟkuvantāṉeṉṉum poruṭṭāy ivveccakkuṟippāy niṟkum. evaṟṟukku eṉpatum atu.
ivviṉiyeccaṅkaṭku eñci niṟkum viṉikaḷum aṟuvakippaṭum avi, terinilimuṟṟuṅ kuṟippumuṟṟum, peyareccamum, peyareccak kuṟippum, viṉiyeccamum, viṉiyeccak kuṟippumām.  ōtivantāṉ - itu terinili muṟṟu; ōti nallaṉ - itu kuṟippu muṟṟu; ōtip peṟṟa poruḷ - itu peyareccam; ōti nalla cāttaṉ - itu peyareccak kuṟippu; uḻutu payaṉkoṇṭu - itu viṉiyeccam; uḻutu aṉṟi uṇṇāṉ - itu viṉiyeccak kuṟippu.	(31)
231.	piṉmuṉ kālkaṭi vaḻiyiṭat teṉṉu
	maṉṉa marapiṟ kālaṅ kaṇṇiya
	veṉṉa kiḷaviyu mavaṟṟiyal piṉavē.
ituvum orucār viṉiyeccam uṇarttukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) piṉ muṉ kāl kaṭi vaḻiiṭattu eṉṉuṅ kiḷaviyum - piṉṉum muṉṉum kālum kaṭiyum vaḻiyum iṭattum eṉṉum īṟṟavākiya coṟkaḷum, aṉṉa marapiṟ kālam kaṇṇiya eṉṉa kiḷaviyum - am muṟimipōlak kālattik kuṟitta ellāc coṟkaḷum, avaṟṟu iyalpiṉa - muṟkūṟiya oṉpatum pōla viṉiyeccattiṟku vāypāṭām iyalpiyuṭiya, e-ṟu.
(u-m.) piṉ : nīyir poy kūṟiyapiṉ mey kūṟuvār yār? ‘iḷamiyun taruvatō viṟanta piṉṉē?’ eṉa iṟappum, nī yivvāṟu kūṟukiṉṟapiṉ urippatu uṇṭō? eṉa nikaḻvumpaṟṟi varum. muṉ: ‘varumuṉṉark kāvātāṉ vāḻkki’  eṉa iṟappuppaṟṟi varum. ivi, piṉṉar, piṉṉi, muṉṉar, muṉṉi eṉat tirintum varum. kāl: ‘valaṉāka viṉiyeṉṟu vaṇaṅkinām viṭuttakkāl’yeṉa iṟappum, ‘akaṉṟavar tiṟattiṉi nāṭuṅkāl’ eṉa nikaḻvum etirvumpaṟṟi varum. kaṭi: ‘toṭarkūrat tuvvāmi vantak kaṭi’yeṉa iṟappuppaṟṟi varum. vaḻi: ‘viṭuvaḻi viṭuvaḻic ceṉṟāṅkavar, toṭuvaḻit toṭuvaḻi nīṅkiṉṟāṟ pacappē’yeṉa iṟappum, ‘avaḷ āṭuvaḻi āṭuvaḻi akalēṉ maṉṉē!’yeṉa nikaḻvum, urikkum vaḻiyeṉa nikaḻvum etirvumpaṟṟi varum. iṭam: ‘kaḷiyunar kikolluṅ kāḻtta viṭattu’yeṉa iṟappum, urikkumiṭattu eṉa nikaḻvum etirvumpaṟṟi varum.
aṉṉamarapiṉ eṉṟataṉāṟ pāṉ, pākku, vāṉ, vākku eṉpaṉavuṅ koḷka. ‘alippāṉ piṟituyiri ākkalum kuṟṟam’ ‘tiruviṟṟāṉ māri kaṟpāṉ tuvalināṭ ceyvatē pōl’ ‘puṇartaru celvam tarupākkuc ceṉṟār’  ‘koḷvāṉ koṭittāṉi koṇṭeḻuntāṉ’ koḷvākku vantāṉ eṉa varum. allatu, allāl eṉa varuṅ kuṟippeccamuṅ koḷka. ivi peyarttaṉmiyum uṭiyavāy niṉṟu muṉvanta viṉiyiṉ kālatti uṇartti niṟṟaliṉ, vēṟu ōtiṉār. ‘vēpākkaṟintu’  eṉpatu vētali aṟinteṉṉum poruḷ tarutaliṉ, eccamaṉṟu. ivaṟṟik ‘kāla kaṇṇiya’ eṉṟatu, kūtir pōyapiṉ vantāṉeṉavum, niṉṟa viṭattu niṉṟāṉ, eṉavum piṉ mutaliyaṉa peyareccattōṭu vantuḻi iṟappu mutaliyaṉa kaṇṇāmiyiṉ.	(32)
232.	avaṟṟuḷ,
	mutaṉili mūṉṟum viṉimutaṉ muṭipiṉa.
itu, mutal niṉṟa mūṉṟaṟkum muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) avaṟṟuḷ mutaṉili mūṉṟum - muṟkūṟiya patiṉintiṉuḷ mutaṟkaṇ niṉṟa ‘ceytu, ceyyū, ceypu’ eṉṉum mūṉṟum, viṉimutal muṭipiṉa - avvecca viṉiyi nikaḻttiṉa karuttāviṉatu viṉiyiṉi uṇarttuñ colliṉiyē muṭipākak koṇṭu muṭitaliyuṭiya, e-ṟu.
(u-m.) uṇṭu vantāṉ; uṇṇū vantāṉ; uṇkupu vantāṉ eṉavum, kaṟṟu vallaṉayiṉāṉ; kallū vallaṉāyiṉāṉ; kaṟkupu vallaṉāyiṉāṉ eṉavum varum.
oṉṟeṉa muṭittal eṉpataṉāṉ, ceyyā eṉpataṟkum uṇṇā vantāṉ, eṉa immuṭipu koḷka.
iṉi, ceyteṉeccak kuṟippākiya iṉṟi, aṉṟi eṉpaṉavun ‘tammiṉ ṟamiyā nannayan taruḷi’‘tolleḻil varittaṉṟi vayavu nōy nalitaliṉ’ eṉa viṉimutal viṉiyāṉ muṭiyum.	(33)
233.	ammuk kiḷaviyuñ ciṉiviṉi tōṉṟiṟ
	ciṉiyoṭu muṭiyā mutaloṭu muṭiyiṉum
	viṉiyō raṉiya veṉmaṉār pulavar.
ituvum ammūṉṟaṟkum muṭipuvēṟṟumi kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) ammukkiḷaviyum - ‘viṉimutal muṭipiṉa’ eṉṟa ammūṉṟu collum, ciṉiviṉi tōṉṟiṟ ciṉiyoṭu muṭiyā - ciṉipporuḷiṭattu viṉiyeccamāyt tōṉṟiṉ tamakku uriya ciṉiviṉi koṇṭu muṭiyā; mutalviṉi koṇṭē muṭiyum; (viṉi) mutaloṭu muṭiyiṉum - avveccaṅkaḷ tām tattamakku uriya viṉimutalāṉ aṉṟip piṟa viṉimutalāṉ muṭiyiṉum piṟavāṟṟāṉ muṭiyiṉum, ōraṉiya eṉmaṉār pulavar- tattamakku uriya viṉimutal koṇṭa taṉmiyōṭu oru taṉmiyi uṭiya eṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar, e-ṟu.
ciṉiyoṭu muṭiyā eṉavē, mutaloṭu muṭital peṟṟām. ummi etirmaṟi.
(u-m.) kaṇṇontu kiṭantāṉ; vayiṟu nontu kiṭantāṉ eṉavum, kaṇ valiyūk kiṭantāṉ eṉavum, vayiṟu kuttupu kiṭantāṉ eṉavum, ‘kaṇṭuyiṉṟu muṉṟiṟpōkā mutirviṉaḷ yāyum’ ‘tāḷoṟṟit tappi vīḻntār taṟivali māṉiṟ paṭṭār’ eṉavuñ ciṉipporuṭkaṇ vanta viṉiyeccam kāraṇa kāriyap poruḷavāy viṉimutalōṭu muṭintaṉa. ‘akaṉamarntu ceyyāḷuṟiyum mukaṉamarntu, nalvirun tōmpuvāṉil’ ‘kāmaṉ kaṇiyoṭu kaṇcivantu pulantāḷ’ eṉpaṉa kāraṇa kāriya miṉṟi aṅṅaṉam vantaṉa. iṉi, talivaṉ pirintu varuntiṉāḷ eṉavum, ‘māal yāṉi yoṭu maṟavar mayaṅkit tūṟatar paṭṭavāṟumayaṅ karuñcuram’ eṉavum, ‘uṭampuyaṅki yāṉi, kaṭuntām patipāṅkuk kiteṟap paṭṭu, veṟiniri vēṟākac cāraccāraṭi, neṟimayaṅkuṟṟa’  eṉavuṅ kāraṇa kāriyapporuḷavāyt tamakku uriya viṉi mutalāṉaṉṟip piṟa viṉimutalāṉ muṭintaṉa. ñāyiṟu paṭṭu vantāṉ; ñāyiṟu paṭūu vantāṉ; ñāyiṟu paṭupu vantāṉ eṉpaṉa kāraṇa kāriyamiṉṟi aṅṅaṉam vantaṉa. ‘uraṟkāl yāṉiyoṭit tuṇṭeñciya yā’ eṉpatuyātu. iṉi, ā kiṭantu ceṟu viḷintatu eṉṟatu, iṭattu nikaḻ poruḷiṉ toḻil iṭattiṟkēṟṟic ceṟuviṉ viṉiyoṭu muṭintatu. itaṟku viṉimutal tāṉē ceyappaṭuporuḷāyiṟṟu. ‘pūṇaṇintu viḷaṅkiya pukaḻcāṉmārpu eṉa aṇinteṉṉum mutalviṉi ciṉiviṉi koṇṭatu. uṇṭu paci keṭṭatum atu. ivi kāraṇa kāriyap poruṭṭāyp piṟavāṟṟāṉ muṭintaṉa.
ēṉi viṉiyeccaṅkaṭku viṉimutal viṉiyum piṟaviṉiyum oppa uriyavāy varutaliṉ, oru cūttirattāṟ kūṟiṉār. immūvakiyeccamum viṉimutal koḷḷuṅkāṟ kāraṇakāriyap poruṭṭākātu varutaliṉ, ataṉiyum vēṟu kūṟi, ciṉiviṉi mutalviṉiyōṭu muṭiyuṅkāl perumpāṉmi kāraṇa kāriyap poruṭṭāyē varuta lāṉum, mutalviṉi tāṉ piṟa viṉimutalāṉ muṭiyumvaḻip perum pāṉmi kāraṇa kāriyap poruṭṭāyē varutalāṉum, avaṟṟi iraṇṭu cūttirattāṟ kūṟiṉār. ‘viṉiyeñcu kiḷaviyum vēṟupal kuṟiya’eṉṟa cūttirattāṉ viṉiyeccaṅkaḷ ellām peyar vēṟupaṭṭum īṟu vēṟupaṭṭuñ ciṟupāṉmi varumeṉṟu potuvākak kūṟiṉārātaliṉ, ataṉkaṇṇē piṟa viṉimutal koṇṭaṉavaṟṟiyum aṭakkutal poruntātu. eṉṉi? avi vaḻakkiṭattuñceyyuḷiṭattum perum pāṉmi varutalāṉum, ‘viṉimutal muṭipiṉa’ eṉṟa viti piṟa viṉimutal koḷḷāmiyi valiyuṟuttu niṟṟaliṟ koḷḷutaṟkuc cūttiraviti vēṇṭu mātalāṉum eṉpatu.	(34)
234.	ēṉi yeccam viṉimuta lāṉu
	māṉavan tiyiyum viṉinili yāṉun
	tāmiyaṉ maruṅkiṉ muṭiyu meṉpa.
iḵtu, oḻinta eccaṅkaṭku muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) ēṉi eccam - mutaṉili mūṉṟum allāta piṟa viṉi yeccam, viṉimutalāṉum - viṉimutal viṉiyāṉum, āṉvantu iyiyum viṉiniliyāṉum - āṇṭu vantu poruntum piṟa viṉiyāṉum, tām iyal maruṅkiṉ muṭiyum eṉpa - variyaṟiyiṉṟit tām iyalumāṟ ṟāṉ muṭiyumeṉṟu colluvar āciriyar, e-ṟu.
(u-m.) maḻi peyteṉap pukaḻ peṟṟatu; maḻi peyteṉa maram kuḻittatu eṉavum, maḻipeyyiyareḻuntatu; maḻi peyyiyar pali koṭuttaṉar eṉavum, maḻi peyyiya muḻaṅkum; maḻi peyyiya vāṉ paḻiccutum eṉavum, maḻi peyyiṟ pukaḻ peṟum; maḻi peyyiṟ kuḷam niṟiyum eṉavum, maḻi peyyap pukaḻ peṟṟatu; maḻi peyya maraṅ kuḻittatu eṉavum, maḻi peytaṟku muḻaṅkum; maḻi peytaṟkuk kaṭavuḷ vāḻttutum eṉavum, iṟantapiṉiḷami vārātu; kaṇavaṉiṉitu uṇṭa piṉ kātali mukam malarntatu eṉavum, kaṭut tiṉṉāmuṟuvarttatu; maruntutiṉṉāmuṉ nōy tīrntatu eṉavum, urittakkāluri palkum; ‘valaṉāka viṉiyeṉṟu vaṇaṅkinām viṭuttakkāṟ cuṭariḻāy namakkavar varutumeṉ ṟurittati’ eṉavum, nalviṉitāṉuṟṟakkaṭi utavum; nalviṉitāṉuṟṟakkaṭi tīviṉi vārā eṉavum, nalviṉi tāṉuṟṟavaḻi utavum; nalviṉitāyuṟṟavaḻit tīviṉi vārā eṉavum, nalviṉitāṉuṟṟa viṭattu utavum; nalviṉitāṉuṟṟaviṭattut tīviṉi vārā eṉavum varum. kaṟpāṉūl ceytāṉ; celvam tarupākku yām virumputum; namatu nalaṉ nukarvāṉ yām virumputum ivi piṟa viṉi koṇṭaṉa. ivi viṉimutal viṉikoṇṭavāṟu, muṉṉark kāṭṭiṉām.	(35)
235.	paṉmuṟi yāṉum viṉiyeñcu kiḷavi
	coṉmuṟi muṭiyā taṭukkuna variṉu
	muṉṉatu muṭiya muṭiyumaṉ poruḷē.
iḵtu, avveccaṅkaḷ aṭukkiyavaḻip paṭuvatoru muṟimi kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) viṉiyeñcu kiḷavi coṉmuṟi muṭiyātu - viṉiyeccamākiya coṟkaḷ oṉṟaṟkoṉṟu muṭikkum viṉivantu muṭiyātu, paṉmuṟiyāṉum aṭukkuna variṉum - oru vāypāṭṭāṉaṉṟip pala vāypāṭṭāṉum aṭukkivariṉum, muṉṉatu muṭiya muṭiyum maṉ poruḷē - muṉṉiṉṟa eccam muṭiya ēṉiyavum poruḷ muṭintaṉavām, e-ṟu.
(u-m.) uṇṭu tiṉṟōṭip pāṭi vantāṉ; uṇṭu parukūut tiṉkupu vantāṉ; uḻutuṇpāṉoṭu virii vantāṉ eṉa varum. ‘pacanta mēṉiyoṭu paṭaraṭa varunti - maruṅkiṟ koṇṭa palkuṟu mākkaḷ - picintutiṉa vāṭiya muliyaḷ peritaḻintu - kuppik kīri koytaṉaṇakitta - muṟṟā viḷantaḷir koytukoṇ ṭuppiṉṟu - nīruliyākavēṟṟiṟōriṉ - ṟaviḻppatam maṟantu pācaṭaku micintu - mācoṭu kuṟinta uṭukkiyavuṟampaḻiyāt - tuvvā ḷākiya ḷaṉvey yōḷum’ eṉac ceyteṉeccattiṭiyē avviṉiyeccamuṟṟum ataṉ kuṟippeccamum uṭaṉ aṭukki muṭintaṉavum piṟavum ‘paṉmuṟi yāṉum’ eṉṟa taṉāṟ koḷka.
viṉiyeccam paṉmuṟiyāṉ aṭukki oru collāṉ muṭiyumeṉavē, peyareccam oru muṟiyāṉ aṭukki oru collāṉ muṭiyumeṉpatām. ‘nelariyumiruntoḻuvar’ eṉṉum puṟappāṭṭiṉuṭ pāyuntu, tūṅkuntu, tarūuntu, pāyumeṉṉum peyareccam aṭukki miḻali eṉṉum orupeyarkoṇṭu muṭintaṉa. āṇṭu nallūr keḻī iya eṉṉum peyareccam iṭiniliyāy vantatu.	(36)
236.	nilaṉum poruḷuṅ kālamuṅ karuviyum
	viṉimutaṟ kiḷaviyum viṉiyu muḷappaṭa
	vavvaṟu poruṭkumō raṉṉa vurimiya
	ceyyuñ ceyta veṉṉuñ collē.
itu ceyyuñceyta eṉṉum peyareccattiṟkup poruṇmuṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) ceyyuñceyta eṉṉuñcollē - ceyyu ceyta eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷ iṉṉataṟku, itu payaṉ eṉṉum iraṇṭoḻittu nilaṉum poruḷum kālalum karuviyum viṉimutaṟkiḷaviyum viṉiyum uḷappaṭa avvaṟu poruṭkuṅ ōraṉṉa urimiya - nilapporuḷumō ceyappaṭuporuḷum  kālapporuḷum karuvip poruḷum viṉimutaṟ poruḷu meṉṟa aintuṭaṉē viṉipporuḷuṅkūṭac collappaṭṭa avvaṟu vakip poruṭkum otta urimiyiṉi uṭiyavām, e-ṟu.
(u-m.) vāḻumil, kaṟkuṅ nūl, tuyiluṅ kālam, vaṉiyukōl, ōtum pārppāṉ, uṇṇumuṇ eṉa um īṟu kāla eḻuttup peṟātu āṟuporuṭkum urittāy vantatu. pukka vil, uṇṭa cōṟu, vanta nāḷ, veṉṟa vēl, āṭiya kūttaṉ, pōyiṉa pōkku eṉa akarīṟu ka, ṭa, ta, ṟa vum yakara ṉakaramum ūrntu, āṟu poruṭkum urittāy vantatu. ivaṟṟuḷ viṉimutaṟkiḷavi oḻintaṉavaṟṟiṟku viṉimutaṟ poruḷ muṉ vantē piṉvarum viṉiyōṭu muṭintu poruḷ tarutalum, viṉimutaṟ kiḷaviyāyiṉ piṉvarum viṉiyoṭu muṭintē poruḷtarutaluṅ koḷka. ivaṟṟiṟkuc ceyyāniṟkum eṉavum ceyyāniṉṟa eṉavum varum vāypāṭṭu vēṟṟumi ‘oṉṟeṉa muṭittalāṟ koḷka. ‘maṟṟinnōy tīruyo maruntaruḷāyoṇ ṭoṭī niṉmuka kāṇu maruntiṉeṉṉumāl’ eṉpuḻi, nōy tīrtaṟkuk kāraṇamākiya maruntu eṉa varuṅ kāraṇattik karuvikkaṇṇum, niṉ mukattik kāṇṭal kāraṇamāka ataṉ kāriyamākap piṟanta aruḷi maruntātal taṉmiyāka uṭiyaṉeṉa varuṅkāriyatti oṉṟeṉa muṭittaṟkaṇṇum amittukkoḷka. ‘tēroṭum puṟaṅkāṇēṉēṉ eṉpatūuṅ ‘nilampūtta maramāyiṉ’ eṉpatūum kāraṇattiṉkaṇṇum, ‘āṟuceṉṟa viyar’ eṉṟataṉi āṟu cēṟalāṉ vanta viyar eṉak kāriyattiṉkaṇṇum aṭakkuka. ‘ārkaḷiṟu mititta nīr’ eḷ ḷāṭṭiṉa eṇṇey; uṇṭa eccil eṉpaṉavuṅ kāriyattiṉkaṇ aṭakkuka. ‘kuṇṭu cuṉip pūtta vaṇṭu paṭu kaṇṇi’ ‘nūlāk kaliṅkam vālarik koḷīi’ eṉpaṉa, ‘pūtta pūvāṉ iyaṉṟa kaṇṇi, nūlāta nūlāṉ iyaṉṟa kaliṅkam eṉa oṟṟuminayam paṟṟic ceyappaṭuporuṇmēl niṉṟaṉa. ‘poccāvāk karuviyāṉ pōṟṟic ceyiṉ.’ eṉpatu karuvikkaṇ aṭaṅkum. aracaṉ ā koṭukkum pārppāṉ, aracaṉ ā koṭutta pārppāṉ eṉavum, āṭi olikkuṅkūli; āṭi olitta kūli eṉavum otta urimiya aṉṟic ciṟupāṉmi iṉṉataṟku, itupayaṉ, eṉpaṉavaṟṟiṉkaṇṇum varum. iṉṉum, paḻam utiruṅ kōṭu, paḻam utirnta kōṭu eṉac ciṟu pāṉmi tīrtaṟ poruṇmi paṟṟi varutaluṅ koḷka. iṉi, ceyta eṉpataṉ kuṟippāy, iṉṉa, aṉṉa, eṉṉa eṉavum, kariya, ceyya eṉavum varuvaṉavum koḷka. avi, ‘iṉṉa taṉmiyi ṉarumiyiṉ’ ‘aṉṉa taṉmiyum aṟintīyār’ eṉavum, ‘eṉṉa kiḷaviyum paṇpiṉ tokiyē’  eṉavum, kariya malar neṭuṅkaṇ eṉavum, ‘ceyya kōli eṉavum vantavāṟu kāṇka.	(37)

237.	avaṟṟoṭu varuvaḻic ceyyumeṉ kiḷavi
	mutaṟkaṇ varinta mūvīṟṟu murittē.
itu, ceyyumeṉpataṟkōr muṭipu vēṟṟumi kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) avaṟṟoṭu varuvaḻi - annilam mutaliya poruḷkaḷoṭu varuṅkāl, ceyyumeṉ kiḷavi - ceyyum meṉṉuñ col, mutaṟkaṇ varinta mūvīṟṟum urittu - mēl vilakkappaṭṭa pallōr paṭarkki, muṉṉili, taṉmi eṉṉum mūvakikkum urittu, e-ṟu.
avi ‘eñciya kiḷavi’ eṉpataṉuṭ kāṭṭiṉām.
īṇṭuk kūṟiya viti eccattiṟkum āṇṭu vilakkiyatu muṟṟiṟkumeṉṟu uṇarka. iṉi, avaṟṟiṟku vēṟupāṭu kūṟutum: irutiṇikkum potuvākiya ceyyumeṉṉuñ col, uṇṇum eṉa niṉṟuḻit tāṉē taṉ toṭarpporuḷu uṇarttalāṟṟātu niṉṟāṉṉi avaṉ, atu eṉṉum viṉimutal vantu tiṇiyum pālum viḷakkat taṉ toṭarpporuḷ uṇartti, eṭuttaṟōciyāṉ maṟṟōru col nōkkātu, ceppu mūyiṉāṟpōla amintu māṟi niṟṟal muṟṟiṟku ilakkaṇam. acceyyumeṉṉuñ col vāḻumil eṉap paṭuttalōciyāṟ kūṟiyavaḻi, ammuṭikkuñ collāṉ amiyātu, naṉṟeṉa maṟṟumōru col nōkki niṟṟal eccattiṟku ilakkaṇam.
iṉi, ammīṟu mutaliya muṟṟuc coṟkaḷ eṅṅaṉam muṟṟi niṟkumeṉiṉ, ‘ettiṟat tāṉum peyarmuṭi piṉavē’  eṉpataṉāṉ viṉimutalōṭu muṭintu, īṇṭuk kūṟiyavāṟē muṟṟi niṟkumeṉka. peyarpaṟṟi nillātu, ‘tāmēyum toṭarpporuḷuṇartti muṟṟiniṟkumeṉac cēṉāvariyar kūṟiṉārāleṉiṉ, ‘cāttaṟkuccōṟu iṭuka!’ eṉa oruvaṉ ēviya vaḻi avvēvappaṭṭāṉ avaṟku ataṉi īttuvantu uṇṭāṉeṉṟa vaḻi, yāṉkūṟiya cāttaṉuṇṭāṉ eṉa viṉimutaṟ toṭarp poruḷ avaṉ maṉattiṉkaṇ uṇarttiniṟpatallatu, naṉmakkaḷ kuḻīiyavaḻi oruvaṉ vantuniṉṟu uṇṭāṉeṉṟavaḻi avviṉimutaṟoṭarp poruḷuṇarttātu pālmāttiram uṇartti niṟkumeṉpatu karuti, ‘ettiṟat tāṉum peyarmuṭi piṉavē’ eṉṟār ākaliṉ, atu poruttamiṉṟu eṉka.	(38)
238.	peyareñcu kiḷaviyum viṉiyeñcu kiḷaviyu
	metirmaṟuttu moḻiyiṉum poruṇili tiriyā.
iḵtu, iruvaki eccattiṟkum etirmaṟi viṉiyum muṭivāmeṉkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) peyareñcu kiḷaviyum viṉiyeñcu kiḷaviyum - peyareccamākiya collum viṉiyeccamākiya collum, etirmaṟuttu moḻiyiṉum - ceytal toḻiliṉi etir  maṟuttuc colliṉum, poruḷ nili tiriyā - tattam eccamākiya peyariyum viṉiyiyuṅ koṇṭu allatu amiyāta nilimiyiṉ vēṟupaṭā, e-ṟu.
(u-m.) uṇṇāvillam, uṇṇāc cōṟu, uṇṇāk kālam, vaḷiyāk kōl, ōtāp pārppāṉ; uṇṇāvūṇ eṉa varum. uṇṇā eṉpatu, uṇṇum, uṇṭa eṉṉum iraṇṭaṟkum maṟi. uṇṇāta eṉpatu, uṇṭa eṉpataṟku maṟi. uṇṇātu eṉpatu, uṇṭu, uṇṇū, uṇṇā, uṇkupu eṉpaṉavaṟṟiṟku maṟi. itaṉāṉē, ceypu eṉpatu iṟantakālam uṇarttutal peṟṟām. iṉi, ceyteṉa eṉpatu viṉi mutalōṭu varuvaḻi, ‘maḻi peyyātaṟam peṟātāyiṟṟu,’ eṉac ceyteṉpataṉ maṟiyē varutalum, atu piṟaviṉiyōṭu varuvaḻi, ‘maḻi peyyāmal maraṅ kuḻiyātāyiṟṟu’, eṉa iṟanta kālam uṇarttuñ ceyteṉeccattu etirmaṟiyē taṉakku maṟiyāy varutaluṅ koḷka. ivviraṇṭu eccattiṟkum ceyyāmi eṉpatūum maṟi. iṉi, uṇṇāmikku eṉpatu, uṇṇiyar, uṇṇiya, uṇṇaṟku eṉpaṉavaṟṟiṟkum, uṇṇa eṉa etirkālam uṇarttuñ ceyaveṉ eccattiṟkum maṟiyām. iṉi, ceyiṉeṉpataṟku, maḻi peyyāviṭiṉaṟam peṟātu, maḻi peyyāviṭiṉ maram kuḻiyātu eṉap piṟa collāṉ maṟi varum. iṉi, uṇṇātapiṉ, uṇṇātamuṉ, uṇṇākkāl, uṇṇāk kaṭi, uṇṇāvaḻi, uṇṇā viṭattu eṉavum varum. iṉi, koḷḷātoḻivāṉ eṉa ēṉiyavaṟṟiṟkum varum. iṉi, kariyacāttaṟkuc ceyya cāttaṉ eṉavum, nalla cāttaṟkup pollāc cāttaṉ eṉavum varum peyareccak kuṟippu maṟi vikaṟpamum aṟika. iṉi, cōṟu uṇṭāyiruntatu, cōṟāvatāyiruntatu, eṉpaṉavaṟṟiṟkuc cōṟiṉṟi, cōṟāṉṟi eṉa viṉiyeccak kuṟippu maṟiyātaluṅ koḷka. ‘etirmaṟuttu moḻiyiṉum tattam marapiṟ, poruḷnili tiriyā vēṟṟumic collē’ eṉṟu vēṟṟumiyiṉi etirmaṟuttu varumeṉavē, avvēṟṟumi ēṟṟa col muṟṟuccollum ātaliṉ, muṟṟuccoṟkum maṟi varumeṉpatu kūṟiṉārāyiṟṟu. iṉi, ceyyumeṉṉum muṟṟiṟkum uṇṇāḷavaḷ, uṇṇātatu eṉa varum maṟi, oṉṟeṉa muṭittalāṟ koḷka.	(39)
239.	tatta meccamoṭu civaṇuṅ kuṟippi
	ṉeccol lāyiṉu miṭinili variyār.
iḵtu, avveccaṅkaḷiṉ iṭinikaḻum muṭipu vēṟṟumi kūṟukiṉṟatu. 
(i-ḷ.) tattam eccamoṭu civaṇuṅ kuṟippiṉ eccollāyiṉum- tattam eccamākiya viṉiyoṭum peyaroṭum poruḷ iyiyuṅ karuttiṉiyuṭiya evvakic collāyiṉum, iṭinili variyār - av veccattiṟkum avaṟṟāṉ muṭivaṉavākiya tamakkum iṭiniṟṟali nīkkār koḷvar āciriyar, e-ṟu.
(u-m.) uḻutu cāttaṉ vantāṉ; uḻutēroṭu vantāṉ; kolluṅ kāṭṭuḷ yāṉi; koṉṟa kāṭṭuḷ yāṉi eṉa varum.
orutaliyāka eccattōṭu iyiyātu, muṭikkappaṭuñ collōṭum iyintu kavarporuḷpaṭuvaṉa civaṇāk kuṟippiṉavām. avi, uṇṭu viruntoṭu vantāṉ; vallameṟinta nalliḷaṅkōcar tanti mallal yāṉip peruvaḻuti eṉpuḻi, viruntōṭu uṇṭeṉavum, vallameṟinta kōcar eṉavuṅ kavarporuṭ paṭṭu varum.
‘eccol lāyiṉum’ eṉṟataṉāl, uḻutōṭi vantāṉ; kavaḷam koḷḷākaḷitta yāṉi eṉa eccam iṭiniliyātal koḷka. aṟattiyaracaṉ virumpiṉāṉ; uṇṭāṉ pacitta cāttaṉ eṉa ēṉit toṭarkkaṇṇum piṟacol iṭiniṟṟal oṉṟeṉa muṭittalāṟ koḷka.	(40)
240.	avaṟṟuḷ,
	ceyyu meṉṉum peyareñcu kiḷavikku
	meyyoṭuṅ keṭumē yīṟṟumici yukara
	mavviṭa ṉaṟita leṉmaṉār pulavar.
itu, ceyyumeṉpataṟku īṟukeṭumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) avaṟṟuḷ - muṟkūṟiya iruvakiyeccaṅkaḷuḷ, ceyyumeṉṉum peyareñcu kiḷavikku - ceyyumeṉṉum peyareccattiṟku, īṟṟumici ukaram meyyoṭuṅkeṭum - īṟṟeḻuttiṉmēl niṉṟa ukaran taṉṉāṉ ūrappaṭṭa meyyoṭuṅ keṭum; avviṭaṉ aṟital eṉmaṉār pulavar - ak keṭumiṭam aṟika, eṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar, e-ṟu.
(u-m.) ‘vāvum puravi vaḻutiyō ṭemmiṭi’ ‘yāṉ pōkum puḻi’ eṉpaṉa  vāmpuravi, pōmpuḻi eṉa niṉṟaṉa.
‘ceyyumeṉṉum peyareñcu kiḷavikku, īṟṟumici ukaram meyyoṭuṅ keṭum,’ eṉavē, ceyyumeṉṉum muṟṟuccoṟku īṟṟumici ukaram meyyoṭuṅ keṭum, mey yoḻittuṅ keṭumeṉpatu peṟutum. (u-m.) ‘ampalurum avaṉoṭu moḻimē.’ (kuṟun. 51) ‘cārama nāṭaveṉ tōḻiyum kaluḻmē’ eṉa varum.	(41)
241.	ceyte ṉeccat tiṟanta kāla
	meytiṭa ṉuṭittē vārāk kālam.
iḵtu, iṟantakālac col ēṉikkālac collōṭu iyiyu meṉkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) ceyteṉeccattu iṟanta kālam - ceyteṉeṉṉum viṉiyeccattiṉatu iṟantakālan taṉ iṟanta kālattiṉi nīṅkātu niṉṟu, vārāk kālam eytiṭaṉ uṭittu - muṭikkuñ colliṉ etir kālattip poruntum iṭamuṭittu, e-ṟu.
(u-m.) nī uṇṭu varuvāy eṉpatu nāḷi uṇṭu varuvāy eṉṉum poruḷ uṇarttavum, varutaṟṟoḻiṟku uṇṭaṟ ṟoḻil muṉ nikaḻntu, āṇṭum taṉ iṟanta kālamē uṇarttiṟṟām. koṭiyāṭit tōṉṟum eṉpuḻit tōṟṟamum āṭṭamum uṭaṉikaḻtaliṉ, ceyteṉpatu nikaḻkālattiṟkum urittātal vārākkālameṉavē aṭaṅkum, ‘vaṟitiṉīnti nīcellum nīḷiṭi niṉippavum’ (kali. 3 : 2) eṉpatum atu.
oṉṟeṉa muṭittalāṟ ceyyū, ceypu, eṉpaṉavaṟṟiṟkum oḻinta eccaṅkaṭkum immayakkaṅ koḷka.	(42)
242.	munnilik kālamun tōṉṟu miyaṟki
	yemmuṟic collu nikaḻuṅ kālattu
	meynnilip potuccoṟ kiḷattal vēṇṭum.
itu, nikaḻkālac col ēṉik kālaṅkaḷiyum uṇarttumeṉkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) munnilik kālamum tōṉṟum iyaṟki emmuṟic collum - mūṉṟu vakippaṭṭa nilimiyiṉi yuṭiya kālattum uḷatām iyalpiyuṭiya evvakippaṭṭa poruḷkaḷiyum, nikaḻuṅ kālattu meynnilip potuccoṟ kiḷattal vēṇṭum - nikaḻkālattiṟku urittāy niṉṟum ēṉik kālaṅkaḷiyum uḷḷaṭakki niṟkumporuḷ nilimiyiṉi yuṭiya ceyyum eṉṉuñ collāṟ collutali virumpum āciriyaṉ, e-ṟu.
mukkālattiṟkum potuvāy muṟṟum eccamumāy niṟṟali nōkkip ‘potuccolleṉṟār.
(u-m.) mali niṟkum; tīc cuṭum; ñāyiṟiyaṅkum; tiṅkaḷiyaṅkum eṉavum, ‘veṅkatirk kaṉaliyoṭu mativalan tiritarum, taṇkaṭal varippu’  eṉavum varum. ‘tīccuṭumeṉṟāl, paṇṭum iṉṟum mēluñ cuṭum eṉpataṉi oppa viḷakkiyavāṟu kāṇka. ‘meynnili’yeṉṟatu, ēṉi nikaḻkālac collōṭu ovvāmiyi uṇarttutaṟku.	(43)
243.	vārāk kālattu nikaḻuṅ kālattu
	mōrāṅku varūum viṉiccoṟ kiḷavi
	yiṟanta kālattuk kuṟippoṭu kiḷattal
	virinta poruḷa veṉmaṉār pulavar.
itu viriviṉkaṇ etirvum nikaḻvum iṟappoṭu mayaṅkum eṉkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) vārāk kālattum nikaḻuṅ kālattum ōrāṅku varūum viṉiccoṟkiḷavi - etirkālattum nikaḻkālattum oru paṭiyāka varukiṉṟa viṉiccollākiya colliṉ poruṇmiyi, iṟanta kālattuk kuṟippoṭu kiḷattal - toḻil iṟantilavēṉuñ colluvāṉ karuttu vakiyāṟ toḻil iṟantaṉavākak karutik kūṟal, virinta poruḷa eṉmaṉār pulavar - virivu poruṇmiyi uṭiya eṉṟu colluvar āciriyar, e-ṟu.
(u-m.) cōṟu pāṇittavaḻi uṇṇātiruntāṉip pōtal vēṇṭuṅ kuṟiyuṭiyāṉoruvaṉ, ‘iṉṉum uṇṭiliyō?’ eṉṟavaḻi, ‘uṇṭēṉ, pōntēṉ’ eṉṉum. uṇṇāniṉṟāṉum, ‘uṇṭēṉ, pōntēṉ’ eṉṉum.	(44)
244.	mikkataṉ maruṅkiṉ viṉiccoṟ cuṭṭi
	yappaṇpu kuṟitta viṉimutaṟ kiḷavi
	ceyva tilvaḻi nikaḻuṅ kālattu
	meypeṟat tōṉṟum poruṭṭā kummē.
itu, mikkatoṉṟaṉkaṇṇē iṟappum etirvum nikaḻvōṭu mayaṅkumeṉkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) mikkataṉ maruṅkiṉ viṉiccoṟ cuṭṭi - iru viṉikaḷ palavaṟṟuḷḷuñ ciṟanta iruviṉikkaṇṇē nikaḻum viṉiccolli nōkki, appaṇpu kuṟitta viṉimutaṟkiḷavi - tiriviṉṟip payakkum ammikka taṉatu paṇpik kuṟittu varum viṉimutaṟ col, ceyvatu ilvaḻi - cuṭṭic collappaṭuva tōr viṉimutal illāta iṭattu, nikaḻuṅkālattu meypeṟat tōṉṟum poruṭṭu ākummē - nikaḻkālattāṉ yāppuṟat tōṉṟum poruḷiyuṭittām, e-ṟu.
(u-m.) tavañ ceytāṉ, ceyvāṉ; tāyik koṉṟāṉ, kol vāṉ; eṉpaṉa mutaliyaṉa mikka viṉiccol. cuvarkkam pukāniṟpaṉ, nirayam pukāniṟpaṉ eṉpaṉa avvappaṇpu kuṟitta viṉi mutaṟkiḷavi. ivi cuṭṭiyoruvar peyar koḷḷāmal, yāvaṉ tavañceytāṉ avaṉ cuvarkkam pukum; yāvaṉ tāyik koṉṟāṉ avaṉ nirayam pukum eṉavum, oruvaṉ tavañ ceyyiṟ, tuṟakkam pukum tāyik kolliṉ, nirayam pukum eṉavum varum. iṅṅaṉam potuvakiyāṟ kūṟātu, cāttaṉ tavañceytu tuṟakkam pukkāṉ  tavam ceyyiṟ tuṟakkam pukuvaṉeṉac cuṭṭi yoruvar peyar koṇṭavaḻi ēṉik kālaṅkaḷāṟ kūṟappaṭutaliṟ ‘ceyvatil vaḻi’ eṉṟār. viṉiccol eṉṟārēṉum, tavañ ceytāṉ; tāyik koṉṟāṉ eṉa viṉippeyarāy varutaluṅ koḷka.	(45)
245.	ituceyal vēṇṭu meṉṉuṅ kiḷavi
	yiruvayi ṉiliyum poruṭṭā kummē
	taṉpā lāṉum piṟaṉpā lāṉum.
itu, muṟṟuccoṟ poruḷtarum vēṟupāṭu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) itu ceyalvēṇṭumeṉṉuṅkiḷavi - ik kāriyattiṉic ceyal vēṇṭumeṉṟu collappaṭum muṟṟuccol, taṉpālāṉum piṟaṉpālāṉum iruvayiṉili yum poruṭṭu ākummē - akkāriyattiṉic ceyvāṉ taṉṉiṭattum, avaṉ ceyali vēṇṭiyiruppāṉ piṟaṉoru vaṉiṭattumeṉṟa iraṇṭiṭattum nilipeṟum poruṇ miyi uṭiyatām, e-ṟu.
(u-m.) cāttaṉ ōtal vēṇṭum eṉpuḻi, vēṇṭu meṉṉum muṟṟuc col oruvaḻic cāttaṉeṉpatu eḻuvāyāy vēṇṭuṉeṉṉum payaṉiliyōṭu muṭintuḻi avvēṇṭutal cāttaṉatāyuñ cāttaṉeṉṉum eḻuvāy, ōtutalēṉṉum viṉippeyar koṇṭavaḻi iv vēṇṭutal tanti  tāymēlum niṉṟavāṟu kāṇka.	(46)
246.	vaṉpuṟa varūum viṉāvuṭi viṉicco
	letirmaṟut tuṇarttutaṟ kurimiyu muṭittē.
ituvum viṉiccoṟ poruḷpaṭum vēṟupāṭu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) vaṉpuṟa varūum viṉāvuṭi viṉiccol - oruvaṉ iṉṉāṅku urittāṉeṉṟu oruvaṉ maṉaṅ koṇṭiruntavaḻit tāṉ avviṉṉāṅku uriyāmiyi avaṉ maṉattu valiyuṟuttutaṟku varum viṉāviṉiyuṭiya viṉiccol, etirmaṟuttu uṇarttutaṟku urimiyum uṭittu - avvuriyāmiyi uṇarttukiṉṟa nilimiyi maṟuttuttāṉ urittāṉāka uṭampaṭṭamiyi avaṟku uṇarttutaṟku urimiyiṉiyum uṭittu, e-ṟu.
ummi etirmaṟiyākalāṉ, maṟuttal perumpāṉmi; nērtal ciṟupāṉmiyām.
ā, ē, ō eṉpaṉa viṉā. oruvaṉ katattāṉāka kaḷiyāṉāka mayaṅki iṉṉāṅku urittup piṉ teruṇṭavaḻi, avviṉṉāṅku urikkappaṭṭāṉ, nī eṉṉi vitāyeṉṟak kāl, yāṉ vitēṉō? eṉat tāṉ viyāmiyi valiyuṟuttutaṟkuk kūṟiyatutāṉē, appoḻutu vitēṉ; nōkātē eṉa nērntamipaṭavum niṟkum. iṉi, tāṉ vitamiyi valiyuṟuttutaṟku varum ‘vitēṉē?’ eṉṉuñ col vitilēṉeṉṉum etirmaṟiyi uṇarttiniṟkum eṉṟalumām.	(47)
247.	vārāk kālattu viṉiccoṟ kiḷavi
	yiṟappiṉu nikaḻviṉuñ ciṟappat tōṉṟu
	miyaṟkiyun teḷivuṅ kiḷakkuṅ kāli.
iḵtu etirvu, iṟappōṭum nikaḻvōṭum mayaṅkum eṉkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) vārāk kālattu viṉiccoṟkiḷavi - etirkālattiṟku uriya viṉiccollākiya colliṉ poruṇmi, iṟappiṉum nikaḻviṉum ciṟappat tōṉṟum iyaṟkiyum teḷivuṅ kiḷakkuṅkāli - vaḻaṅkalāṟṟāṉ iḵtu ippeṟṟittu  eṉap peṟṟiyāṉ aṟintirunta iyalpākalum, itu nikaḻiṉ atu nikaḻumeṉa nūlneṟiyāṉ teḷiyap paṭutaluñ collumiṭattu iṟantakālac collāṉum nikaḻkālac collāṉum viḷaṅkat tōṉṟum, e-ṟu.
(u-m.) ikkāṭṭuṭ pōkiṉ kūṟi kōṭpaṭuvaṉ eṉaṟpālatu, kūṟi kōṭpaṭṭāṉ, kūṟi kōṭpaṭukiṉṟāṉ eṉak kūṟutal iyaṟki. eṟumpu muṭṭi koṇṭu tiṭṭi yēṟiyatu kaṇṭuḻi maḻi peyvatām eṉṉātu, maḻi peytatu, maḻi peykiṉṟatu eṉak kūṟutal teḷivu.	(48)
248.	ceyappaṭu poruḷic ceytatu pōlat
	toḻiṟpaṭak kiḷattalum vaḻakkiyaṉ marapē.
itu, viṉiccoṟkaṇ marapuvaḻuvamiti kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) ceyappaṭuporuḷi - oru viṉimutal oru toḻil ceyvataṉi uṟuvatākiya poruḷi, ceytatu pōlat toḻiṟpaṭak kiḷattalum - tāṉ attoḻiliṉic ceyta viṉimutal pōla at toḻil ataṉmēṟpaṭak kūṟutalum, vaḻakkiyal marapē - vaḻakkiṉkaṇṇē naṭakkum muṟimi, e-ṟu.
eṉavē, ilakkaṇam aṉṟāyiṟṟu.
(u-m.) illam meḻukappaṭṭatu eṉṉātu, illam meḻukiṟṟeṉṟalām. iṅṅaṉam viṉimutalvāypāṭṭāṟ kiḷattalēyaṉṟi, eḷitiṉ aṭappaṭuvataṉi nōkki, aricitāṉē aṭṭatu eṉac ceyappaṭuporuḷi viṉi mutalākak kūṟalum aṭaṅkutaṟkut ‘toḻiṟpaṭa kiḷattalum’ eṉṟār. ivi karumakkaruttā.
iṉi, oṉṟeṉa muṭittalāṉ, ivvāḷeṟiyum eṉak karuviyit tāṉē ceyvatākak kūṟuṅ karuvikkaruttāvum, aracaṉ eṭutta ālayam eṉa ēviṉāṉik karuttāvākac kūṟum ētukkaruttāvuṅ koḷka.	(49)
249.	iṟappē yetirvē yāyiru kālamuñ
	ciṟappat tōṉṟu mayaṅkumoḻik kiḷavi.
iḵtu, oru poruṇmi kuṟiyātu iṟappum etirvum mayaṅkumeṉkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) iṟappē etirvē āyiru kālamum - iṟappum etirvumākiya iraṇṭu kālamum, mayaṅkum moḻikkiḷavi ciṟappat tōṉṟum - tammuḷ mayaṅkum moḻipporuḷāy viḷaṅkat tōṉṟum, e-ṟu.
(u-m.) ivar paṇṭu ippoḻiliṭattu viḷiyāṭuvar; nāḷi avaṉ vāḷoṭu vekuṇṭu vantāṉ, piṉ nī eṉ ceykuvi? eṉa varum. avi viṉippeyarum viṉiyumāy mayaṅkutaliṉ, mayaṅkum viṉiccoṟ kiḷavi eṉṉātu, potuppaṭa ‘mayaṅkum moḻikkiḷavi’ eṉṟār.	(50)

250.	ēṉik kālamu mayaṅkutal variyār.
itu, nikaḻvu ēṉiyavaṟṟoṭu mayaṅkumeṉkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) ēṉik kālamum mayaṅkutal variyār - nikaḻkālamum iṟappōṭum etirvoṭum mayaṅkutali nīkkār koḷvar āciriyar, e-ṟu.
(u-m.) ivaḷ paṇṭu ippoḻiliṭattu viḷiyāṭum;  nāḷi varum eṉa varum.
mūṉṟu kālamum viṉippeyaroṭu mayaṅkum eṉṟārēṉum, ēṟṟaṉavē koḷka.	(51)
viṉiyiyal muṟṟum



iṭiyiyal
iṭiccoṟkaḷiṉ ilakkaṇamuṇarttiṉamiyāl iṭiyiyaleṉṉum peyarttāyiṟṟu. peyariyum viṉiyiyum cārntu tōṟṟutaliṉ avaṟṟiṉpiṉ kūṟappaṭṭatu moḻikku muṉṉum piṉṉum varumāyiṉum perumpāṉmiyum iṭivarutaliṉ iṭiccollāyiṟṟu eṉpar cēṉāvariyar. iṭiccollāvatu peyarum viṉiyumpōlat taṉittaṉiyē poruḷuṇara uccarikkappaṭātu peyar viṉikaḷic cārntu pulappaṭumeṉṟum peyarum viṉiyum iṭamākaniṉṟu poruḷuṇarttaliṉ iṭiccollāyiṟṟeṉṟum kūṟuvar teyvacciliyār. poruḷiyum poruḷatu puṭi peyarcciyiyun tammālaṉṟit tattaṅkuṟippāluṇarttuñ coṟkaḷ peyarccol viṉiccoṟkaḷumākātu avaṟṟiṉ vēṟumākātu iṭinikaraṉavāy niṟṟaliṉ iṭiccolleṉap paṭṭaṉa eṉpar civañāṉa muṉivar. ivviyaliluḷḷa cūttiraṅkaḷ 48. ivaṟṟi 47-ākak koḷvar teyvacciliyār.
iṭiccolleṉṟu collappaṭuvaṉa peyarum viṉiyum uṇarttum poruḷic cārntuniṉṟu avaṟṟiyē veḷippaṭuttu niṟṟalallatu tamakkeṉa vēṟu poruḷillātaṉ eṉpar āciriyar. eṉavē avi poruḷuṇarttumvaḻip peyarp poruṇmi yuṇarttiyum viṉipporuṇmi yuṇarttiyum varuvaṉa eṉpatu peṟappaṭum.
oru collōṭu oru col puṇarntiyalum vaḻi apporuḷ nilikku utaviyāki varuvaṉavum, viṉic coṟkaḷi muṭikkumiṭattu accollakattuk kālaṅkāṭṭum uṟuppu mutaliyaṉavāy niṟpaṉavum, vēṟṟumiyurupukaḷāy varuvaṉavum, tamakkeṉap poruḷiṉṟic cārttic collappaṭum acinilikaḷāy niṟpaṉavum, ici niṟikka varuvaṉavum, tat taṅkuṟippāṟ poruḷuṇarttuvaṉavum. oppumiyuṇarttum uvamavurupukaḷāy varuvaṉavum eṉa iṭiccoṟkaḷ eḻuvakippaṭumeṉpar tolkāppiyar. avaṟṟuḷ ‘puṇariyal niliyiṭip poruḷ nilikkutavuna’ eṉṟatu alvaḻip poruḷukku uriyaṉa ivi vēṟṟumip poruḷukku uriyaṉa iviyeṉa eḷitil uṇarntu koḷḷutaṟku aṟikuṟiyākiya iṉ, vaṟṟu, mutaliya cāriyikaḷi ivi eḻuttatikārattiṟ collappaṭṭaṉa.
‘viṉi ceyal maruṅkiṟ kālamoṭu varuna’ eṉṟatu viṉiccol muṭivupeṟumiṭattuk kālaṅkāṭṭiyum pālkāṭṭiyum viṉiccollakattu uṟuppāy niṟpaṉavaṟṟi. ivi viṉiyiyaluṭ kūṟappaṭṭaṉa vēṟṟumip poruḷiṭattu urupāy varum ai, oṭu, ku, iṉ, atu, kaṇ eṉavarum vēṟṟumiyurupukaḷ vēṟṟumiyiyaliṟ kūṟappaṭṭaṉa. aciniliyum iciniṟiyum tattaṅ kuṟippiṟ poruḷ ceyvaṉavum ākiya mūvakiyiṭic coṟkaḷum ivviyaliṉ kaṇṇē uṇarttappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. oppumiyuṇarttum iṭic coṟkaḷākiya aṉṉa, āṅku mutaliya uvama urupukaḷ poruḷatikārattu  uvama iyalil viritturikkappaṭum. ivviṭiccoṟkaḷ tammāṟ cārappaṭum coṟku muṉṉum piṉṉum varutalum tammīṟutiritalum piṟitōriṭic col tammuṉvantu cārappeṟutalum ākiya iyalpiṉavām.
ivviyaliṉkaṇ uṇarttappaṭum acinili, iciniṟi tattaṅkuṟippiṟ poruḷceyvaṉa eṉṉum mūvakiyiṭic coṟkaḷuḷ poruḷpuṇar iṭiccollākiya tattaṅkuṟippiṟ poruḷ ceyvaṉavaṟṟi mutaṟkaṇṇum, poruḷpuṇarā iṭiccoṟkaḷākiya acinili iciniṟikaḷi ataṉ piṉṉarum uṇarttukiṉṟār. tattaṅkuṟippiṟ poruḷceyyum iṭic coṟkaḷuḷ palaporuḷ kuṟitta iṭiccoṟkaḷi 4-mutal 12-variyuḷḷa cūttiraṅkaḷilum, oruporuḷ kuṟitta iṭiccoṟkaḷi 13-mutal 21-variyuḷḷa cūttiraṅkaḷilum āciriyar eṭuttōtukiṉṟār. avar eṭuttōtiya iṭiccoṟkaḷuḷ el eṉṉum col ilaṅkutal eṉṉum oru poruḷ kuṟitta iṭiccollākum. “elleṉpatu uriccol nīrmittāyiṉum āciriyar iṭiccollāka otiṉamiyāṉ iṭiccol leṉṟu kōṭum” eṉṟār cēṉāvariyar. ‘uriccol kuṟic collāki niṟkum, itu kuṟiyiṉṟi niṟṟaliṉ iṭic collāyiṟṟu’ eṉṟār teyvacciliyār. aciniliyākavum iciniṟiyākavum varum iṭiccoṟkaḷi 22-mutal 32-variyuḷḷa cūttiraṅkaḷāl eṭutturittār. uyireḻuttukkaḷuḷ oḷakāramallāta neṭilkaḷ āṟum iraṭṭittum aḷapeṭuttum taṉittum iṭiccoṟkaḷāy niṉṟu ōciyālum kuṟippālum poruḷuṇarttum muṟiyiṉi 38-ām cūttirattilum, naṉṟē, aṉṟē, antō, aṉṉō eṉpavaṟṟiṉ iṟuti niṉṟa ēkāramum ōkāramum kuṟippāṟ poruḷuṇarttu muṟiyiṉi 34-ām cūttirattilum, mēṟkūṟiya iṭic colliṉkaṇ ilakkaṇa vēṟupāṭukaḷiyellām 35-mutal 46-variyuḷḷa cūttiraṅ kaḷilum āciriyar virittuk kūṟiyuḷḷār.
mēṟkūṟappaṭṭa iṭiccoṟkaḷil iccollukku itu poruḷ eṉa nilipeṟac collappaṭṭaṉavāyiṉum accoṟkaḷiṉ muṉṉum piṉṉum niṉṟa viṉiyōṭum peyarōṭum iyittu nōkka accoṟkaḷ muṟkūṟiyavāṟaṉṟi vēṟu poruḷavāyum aciniliyāyum tirintu vēṟupaṭiṉum avaṟṟiṉ poruḷ niliyi ārāyntuṇartal vēṇṭu meṉavum, iṅkeṭutturitta iṭiccoṟkaḷēyaṉṟi ivipōlvaṉa piṟa variṉum avaṟṟiyum iṅkuc colliya vaṟṟiṉ ilakkaṇattāl uṇarntu vakippaṭuttuk koḷḷutal vēṇṭumeṉavum ivviyaliṟutiyiluḷḷa puṟaṉaṭic cūttiraṅkaḷāl āciriyar aṟivuṟuttukiṉṟār. itaṉāl āciriyar kālattu vaḻaṅkiya tamiḻccoṟkaḷiṉ parappum iruvaki vaḻakkiṉum coṟkaḷ poruḷuṇarttum neṟiyiṉ virivum iṉitu pulaṉātal kāṇalām.
- ka. veḷḷivāraṇaṉār 
nūlvarici 10, pak. 214-216


ēḻāvatu
iṭiccolliyal
